# YONO Business RAG Chatbot — Environment Variables
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# Copy this file to .env and fill in the required values.
# NEVER commit .env to version control.
#
# All variables use the "SECTION__FIELD" naming convention so that
# pydantic-settings maps them directly into the nested Settings model.
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

# ── Elasticsearch (REQUIRED) ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
# API key for authenticating with the Elasticsearch cluster.
# Obtain from the Elasticsearch Kibana console or cluster administrator.
RAG__ELASTICSEARCH_API_KEY=<your-elasticsearch-api-key-here>

# Optional host override (default is set in config.toml):
# RAG__ELASTICSEARCH_HOST=https://prod-es-host:9201

# Enable TLS cert verification in production:
# RAG__VERIFY_CERTS=true

# ── LLM — DLH self-hosted vLLM (REQUIRED) ────────────────────────────────────
# API key for the DLH vLLM endpoint.
# For unauthenticated endpoints use the literal string: EMPTY
LLM__API_KEY=EMPTY

# Optional overrides (defaults are set in config.toml):
# LLM__MODEL_NAME=google/gemma-4-31b-it
# LLM__BASE_URL=http://10.190.236.15:9000/api/v1
# LLM__VERIFY_SSL=false
# LLM__TEMPERATURE=0.2
# LLM__MAX_TOKENS=1024
# LLM__REQUEST_TIMEOUT=60.0

# ── Common project (optional) ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
# COMMON__PROJECT_ID=yono-chatbot-prod
# COMMON__MLFLOW_EXPERIMENT_NAME=yono-business-rag-v1

# ── Logging (optional) ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# LOGGING__LOG_LEVEL=INFO

# How to Run - YONO Business RAG Chatbot

This guide covers every way to start the application: backend only, UI only, or both together via the Launcher.

---

## Prerequisites

| Requirement | Minimum | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| Python | 3.11+ | Check with `python --version` |
| pip | latest | `pip install --upgrade pip` |
| Elasticsearch | 8.x or 9.x | Must be reachable from your machine |
| HDFS access | - | Required for loading the embedding model at startup |
| DLH vLLM endpoint | - | `http://10.190.236.15:9000/api/v1` or equivalent |
| SBI internal network | - | VPN / intranet required |

---

## 1. Install Dependencies

### Option A - pip (recommended)

```bash
# From the project root (v1/)
pip install -r requirements.txt
```

### Option B - editable install (development)

```bash
pip install -e .
# Also install dev tools (ruff, pytest, pyrefly)
pip install -e ".[dev]"
```

---

## 2. Configure Environment

Copy the example env file and fill in your secrets:

```bash
cp .env.example .env
```

Open `.env` and set at minimum:

```bash
# Required - Elasticsearch API key
RAG__ELASTICSEARCH_API_KEY=<your-elasticsearch-api-key>

# Required - DLH LLM API key (use EMPTY for unauthenticated endpoints)
LLM__API_KEY=EMPTY
```

All other settings have defaults in `src/config.toml`. Override them in `.env` if needed:

```bash
# Optional overrides
RAG__ELASTICSEARCH_HOST=https://uateladata02.dlhdevuat.sbi.bank.in:9201/
LLM__BASE_URL=http://10.190.236.15:9000/api/v1
LLM__MODEL_NAME=gemma-4-26b-a4b-it
LOGGING__LOG_LEVEL=INFO
```

> **Never commit `.env` to version control.** It is already listed in `.gitignore`.

---

## 3. Set PYTHONPATH

The `dlh_ai` package lives under `src/`. Add it to your Python path before running anything:

**Linux / macOS:**
```bash
export PYTHONPATH=src:$PYTHONPATH
```

**Windows (PowerShell):**
```powershell
$env:PYTHONPATH = "src;$env:PYTHONPATH"
```

---

## 4. Run Method A - Backend Only (FastAPI)

Use this when you want to test the API without the Gradio UI, or use the built-in HTML frontend.

```bash
uvicorn dlh_ai.genai.application.backend.main:app \
    --host 0.0.0.0 \
    --port 8008 \
    --reload
```

**Windows PowerShell:**
```powershell
uvicorn dlh_ai.genai.application.backend.main:app --host 0.0.0.0 --port 8008 --reload
```

Once running:

| URL | Description |
|---|---|
| `http://localhost:8008/` | Static HTML chatbot frontend |
| `http://localhost:8008/health` | Health check - returns `{"status":"ok"}` |
| `http://localhost:8008/docs` | Swagger UI - interactive API explorer |
| `http://localhost:8008/redoc` | ReDoc API documentation |
| `http://localhost:8008/api/v1/chat` | Chat endpoint (POST) |

**First-time only - ingest the knowledge base:**
```bash
curl -X POST http://localhost:8008/api/v1/admin/ingest
```

---

## 5. Run Method B - Gradio UI Only

Use this when the backend is already running elsewhere and you only want the Gradio interface.

```bash
# Point the UI at your running backend
export CB_BACKEND_URL=http://127.0.0.1:8008
export CDSW_APP_PORT=8100

python src/dlh_ai/genai/application/ui.py
```

**Windows PowerShell:**
```powershell
$env:CB_BACKEND_URL = "http://127.0.0.1:8008"
$env:CDSW_APP_PORT = "8100"
python src/dlh_ai/genai/application/ui.py
```

Open `http://localhost:8100` in your browser.

---

## 6. Run Method C - Both Together via Launcher (Recommended)

The Launcher starts the FastAPI backend and the Gradio UI as separate processes, wires them together, and monitors both. Press `Ctrl+C` to stop both cleanly.

```bash
python src/dlh_ai/genai/application/Launcher.py
```

The Launcher will:
1. Check that `backend/main.py` and `ui.py` exist.
2. Start the FastAPI backend on port `8008` (default).
3. Wait 10 seconds for the backend to finish loading the embedding model.
4. Start the Gradio UI on port `8100` (default).
5. Print the URLs and monitor both processes.

Expected output:
```
 Checking environment...

 Starting YONO Business Chatbot Suite
============================================================
 Launching Backend (FastAPI) on port 8008...
 Waiting 10 seconds for backend to initialise...
 Launching UI (Gradio) on port 8100...

============================================================
 YONO BUSINESS CHATBOT - SYSTEMS STARTED
  Backend API : http://127.0.0.1:8008
  API Docs    : http://127.0.0.1:8008/docs
  Frontend UI : http://127.0.0.1:8100
============================================================

 Open the Frontend UI link above (or the CML app link) to use the chatbot.
 Press Ctrl+C to stop both servers.
```

### Override ports (optional)

```bash
# Linux / macOS
CB_BACKEND_PORT=9000 CDSW_APP_PORT=9001 python src/dlh_ai/genai/application/Launcher.py

# Windows PowerShell
$env:CB_BACKEND_PORT = "9000"
$env:CDSW_APP_PORT = "9001"
python src/dlh_ai/genai/application/Launcher.py
```

---

## 7. Run Method D - Production (Gunicorn + Uvicorn Workers)

For production deployments, use Gunicorn with multiple Uvicorn worker processes:

```bash
gunicorn dlh_ai.genai.application.backend.main:app \
    -k uvicorn.workers.UvicornWorker \
    --workers 4 \
    --bind 0.0.0.0:8008 \
    --timeout 120
```

> Gunicorn is not included in `requirements.txt` by default. Install it with: `pip install gunicorn`

---

## 8. Ingest the Knowledge Base

The knowledge base must be ingested into Elasticsearch before the chatbot can answer questions. This is a one-time operation (or re-run whenever the CSV is updated).

**Via curl:**
```bash
curl -X POST http://localhost:8008/api/v1/admin/ingest
```

**Via Python:**
```python
import httpx
r = httpx.post("http://localhost:8008/api/v1/admin/ingest")
print(r.json())
# {"total_rows": 1039, "successful": 1039, "failed": 0}
```

---

## 9. Test the Chat API

```bash
curl -X POST http://localhost:8008/api/v1/chat \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"query": "How do I block my debit card?", "top_k": 5}'
```

Expected response:
```json
{
  "query": "How do I block my debit card?",
  "answer": "You can temporarily block your ATM/Debit card through the YONO app...",
  "category": "ATM/Debit Card Service",
  "doc_type": "Main Category",
  "score": 0.9477,
  "sources": [...],
  "session_id": null
}
```

---

## 10. Environment Variables Reference

| Variable | Default | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `RAG__ELASTICSEARCH_API_KEY` | - | Yes | Elasticsearch authentication key |
| `RAG__ELASTICSEARCH_HOST` | `https://uateladata02...` | No | Elasticsearch cluster URL |
| `RAG__VERIFY_CERTS` | `false` | No | Enable TLS cert verification |
| `RAG__INDEX_NAME` | `yono_business_knowledge_base` | No | Elasticsearch index name |
| `LLM__API_KEY` | `EMPTY` | Yes | DLH vLLM API key |
| `LLM__BASE_URL` | `http://10.190.236.15:9000/api/v1` | No | DLH vLLM endpoint URL |
| `LLM__MODEL_NAME` | `gemma-4-26b-a4b-it` | No | Model identifier |
| `LLM__TEMPERATURE` | `0.2` | No | Sampling temperature (0.0-2.0) |
| `LLM__MAX_TOKENS` | `1024` | No | Max tokens in LLM response |
| `CB_BACKEND_PORT` | `8008` | No | FastAPI backend port (Launcher only) |
| `CB_BACKEND_URL` | `http://127.0.0.1:8008` | No | Backend URL (Gradio UI reads this) |
| `CDSW_APP_PORT` | `8100` | No | Gradio UI port |
| `LOGGING__LOG_LEVEL` | `DEBUG` | No | Log level: DEBUG / INFO / WARNING |

---

## 11. Troubleshooting

**Backend fails to start - `ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'dlh_ai'`**
```bash
# Make sure PYTHONPATH includes src/
export PYTHONPATH=src:$PYTHONPATH
```

**Elasticsearch connection refused**
- Confirm `RAG__ELASTICSEARCH_HOST` and `RAG__ELASTICSEARCH_API_KEY` are set correctly in `.env`.
- Check VPN / intranet connectivity to the Elasticsearch cluster.

**Embedding model load fails**
- Confirm HDFS is accessible and the model path in `src/config.toml` is correct.
- Check `logs/app.log` for the full traceback.

**`Backend not responding` warning in Launcher**
- The backend may still be loading the embedding model (can take 30-60 seconds on first start).
- Check the backend terminal window for errors.

**UI shows `Backend Offline` badge**
- The Gradio UI cannot reach `CB_BACKEND_URL`. Make sure the backend is running and the URL is correct.



####################################################################################################
FILE: README.md
####################################################################################################

# YONO Business RAG Chatbot

> **Enterprise-grade Retrieval-Augmented Generation chatbot for the YONO Business digital banking platform — State Bank of India.**
> Designed to handle **10 million concurrent users** via Elasticsearch kNN vector search, DLH self-hosted Gemma-4 LLM, and a FastAPI async backend.

---

## Overview

YONO Business Assistant answers customer banking questions by retrieving the most relevant passages from a structured FAQ knowledge base and synthesising a coherent answer using the DLH self-hosted Gemma-4-26b language model.

```
Customer query
    │
    ▼  encode (all-MiniLM-L12-v2 · 384-dim)
    │
    ▼  kNN search  ─────► Elasticsearch
    │                     yono_business_knowledge_base
    ▼  render (Jinja2 prompts)
    │
    ▼  generate ──────────► DLH vLLM  (Gemma-4-26b-a4b-it)
    │
    ▼
ChatResponse  ──────────► Browser SPA
```

---

## Overall Architecture

```mermaid
flowchart TB
    subgraph Client["Client Layer"]
        Customer["YONO Customer<br/>(Browser)"]
        Admin["SBI Admin / Data Team"]
    end

    subgraph Gateway["Network Layer (SBI-managed, outside this app)"]
        GW["Network Gateway / Reverse Proxy<br/>enforces admin &amp; chat access control<br/>(no app-level auth by design)"]
    end

    subgraph App["YONO Business RAG Chatbot"]
        direction TB
        FE["Frontend SPA<br/>index.html + app.js<br/>(mounted last, CSP-hardened)"]
        API["FastAPI Backend<br/>/api/v1/chat · /api/v1/admin/ingest · /health"]
        GR["Guardrail Service<br/>Heuristic engine — regex PII redaction, better-profanity,<br/>detoxify, banking-topic &amp; grounding checks (no LLM calls)"]
        RAG["RAG Pipeline<br/>EmbeddingService -&gt; RetrievalService -&gt; LlmService"]
        CFG["Config Layer<br/>RagSettings · LlmSettings · GuardrailSettings · ApiSettings"]
    end

    subgraph Ext["External Systems"]
        ES[("Elasticsearch 8.x<br/>kNN + BM25 hybrid search")]
        LLM[("DLH vLLM<br/>Gemma-4-26b-a4b-it")]
        HDFS[("HDFS<br/>embedding model + FAQ CSV")]
    end

    Customer -->|HTTPS| GW
    Admin -->|HTTPS, admin-only| GW
    GW --> FE
    GW -->|"/api/v1/chat"| API
    GW -->|"/api/v1/admin/ingest"| API
    FE -->|"POST /api/v1/chat"| API

    API --> GR
    GR -->|validated query| RAG
    RAG --> GR
    GR -->|validated answer| API

    RAG -->|encode + kNN search| ES
    RAG -->|generate| LLM
    API -->|ingest FAQ CSV| HDFS
    RAG -->|load embedding model| HDFS

    CFG -.->|configures| API
    CFG -.->|configures| GR
    CFG -.->|configures| RAG
```

> For the formal C4 Context/Container/Component diagrams, sequence diagrams, and every other architecture view, see [docs/mermaid.txt](docs/mermaid.txt).

---

## Features

| Capability | Detail |
|---|---|
| **RAG Pipeline** | 4-step: encode → kNN search → prompt render → LLM generate |
| **Vector Search** | Elasticsearch 8.x kNN cosine similarity (384-dim dense vectors) |
| **Hybrid Search** | kNN + BM25 combined for improved recall on uncommon terms |
| **Reranking (optional)** | ONNX-accelerated cross-encoder second-stage reranking (`optimum`, auto-falls back to torch `CrossEncoder`); disabled by default via `[reranker].enabled` |
| **Embedding Model** | `all-MiniLM-L12-v2` (384-dim) loaded from HDFS at startup |
| **LLM** | DLH self-hosted Gemma-4-26b-a4b-it via OpenAI-compatible vLLM API |
| **Backend** | FastAPI + Uvicorn, async with `run_in_executor` for blocking I/O |
| **Frontend** | Full-window dark enterprise SPA — confidence bar, sources accordion, suggestion chips |
| **Config** | `pydantic-settings` — env vars → `.env` → `config.toml` → defaults |
| **Guardrails** | `GuardrailService` — regex-based PII redaction, `better-profanity` profanity filtering, `detoxify` toxicity scoring, and regex/lexical heuristics for prompt-injection, banking-topic enforcement, and hallucination/grounding checks. No LLM calls; wired into every chat request between input validation, retrieval, and output validation |
| **Logging** | `rich` RichHandler (console) + rotating `FileHandler` (`logs/app.log`) |
| **Scale** | Gunicorn multi-worker + Nginx reverse proxy for horizontal scale |
| **Standards** | NumPy docstrings · type hints · Ruff lint · Pyrefly type checking |

---

## Prerequisites

| Requirement | Minimum |
|---|---|
| Python | ≥ 3.11 |
| Elasticsearch | 8.x or 9.x |
| HDFS access | `/dev/aiml/models/sentence-transformers/…` |
| DLH vLLM endpoint | `http://<dlh-vllm-host>:9000/api/v1` (internal DLH endpoint; ask the AI platform team for the current UAT/prod host) |
| Network | SBI internal VPN / intranet access |

---

## Quick Start

```bash
# 1. Clone the repository
git clone <repo-url>
cd yono-business-chatbot/v1

# 2. Create a virtual environment and install dependencies
python3.11 -m venv .venv
source .venv/bin/activate          # Windows: .venv\Scripts\activate
pip install -e .

# 3. Configure secrets
cp .env.example .env
# Edit .env — set at minimum:
#   RAG__ELASTICSEARCH_API_KEY=<your-key>
#   LLM__API_KEY=EMPTY

# 4. Start the development server
uvicorn dlh_ai.genai.application.backend.main:app \
    --host 0.0.0.0 --port 8000 --reload

# 5. Ingest the knowledge base (first time only)
curl -X POST http://localhost:8000/api/v1/admin/ingest

# 6. Open the chatbot UI
#    http://localhost:8000
```

---

## Project Structure

```
v1/
├── README.md                          ← this file
├── pyproject.toml                     ← dependencies + Ruff + Pyrefly config
├── .env.example                       ← template for required environment variables
├── .env                               ← secrets (git-ignored — never commit)
├── logs/
│   └── app.log                        ← application log file (auto-created)
└── src/
    ├── config.toml                    ← non-secret configuration defaults
    └── dlh_ai/
        ├── __init__.py                ← logging setup (RichHandler + FileHandler)
        ├── config.py                  ← pydantic-settings: Settings, RagSettings, LlmSettings, GuardrailSettings, ApiSettings
        ├── utils/
        │   ├── __init__.py
        │   └── _hdfs_client.py        ← HDFSClient (model download from HDFS)
        └── genai/
            ├── __init__.py
            ├── docs/                  ← full documentation (see Documentation section)
            ├── prompt/
            │   ├── __init__.py
            │   ├── _manager.py        ← PromptManager — Jinja2 YAML template loader
            │   └── yono_business_chatbot.yaml  ← system / rag_chat / fallback / greeting / error
            ├── rag/
            │   ├── __init__.py        ← public re-exports
            │   ├── exceptions.py      ← YonoRagError + domain subclasses (incl. GuardrailViolationError, PIIDetectedError)
            │   ├── _models.py         ← Pydantic DTOs (ChatRequest, ChatResponse, SearchHit …)
            │   ├── _es_client.py      ← build_es_client() — connection pool + health check
            │   ├── _embedding_service.py ← EmbeddingService — HDFS model load + encode
            │   ├── _guardrails.py     ← GuardrailService — regex PII redaction, better-profanity, detoxify, banking-topic/injection/grounding heuristics (no LLM calls)
            │   ├── _evaluation.py     ← RagEvaluator / evaluate_dataset — from-scratch heuristic RAG-quality metrics (no ragas, no datasets)
            │   ├── _ingestion.py      ← CSV → embed → helpers.bulk → Elasticsearch
            │   ├── _llm_service.py    ← LlmService — DLH Gemma-4 via openai client
            │   ├── _reranker.py       ← RerankerService — optional ONNX/torch cross-encoder second-stage reranker
            │   └── _retrieval.py      ← RetrievalService — RAG orchestrator (input guardrail → retrieve → prompt → LLM → output guardrail)
            └── application/
                ├── Launcher.py        ← container entry point — spawns the backend (uvicorn) and ui.py as subprocesses
                ├── ui.py              ← stdlib `http.server`-based chat frontend (used in the CML/container launch path)
                ├── backend/
                │   └── main.py        ← FastAPI app: lifespan, /health, /api/v1/chat, /api/v1/admin/ingest (static mount registered LAST)
                └── frontend/
                    ├── index.html     ← dark enterprise SPA chat UI (served via StaticFiles; CSP-protected)
                    └── app.js         ← SPA JavaScript, extracted to a same-origin file so CSP's `script-src 'self'` works
```

---

## Configuration

### `src/config.toml` — non-secret defaults

```toml
[rag]
elasticsearch_host   = "https://uateladata02.dlhdevuat.sbi.bank.in:9201/"
index_name           = "yono_business_knowledge_base"
hdfs_model_path      = "/dev/aiml/models/sentence-transformers/models--sentence-transformers--all-MiniLM-L12-v2/snapshots/a50ef00143b4d5391434df20ae11632588ac25be"
embedding_dimension  = 384
csv_file_path        = "yono_business_knowledge_base_fill_category.csv"
batch_size           = 100
connections_per_node = 20
verify_certs         = false   # UAT self-signed certs only — pydantic default is true; set true (or omit) in production

[reranker]
enabled              = false   # optional cross-encoder second-stage reranking; off until a model is staged on HDFS
model_path           = "/dev/aiml/models/mixedbread-ai/mxbai-rerank-large-v2/"
use_onnx             = true    # ONNX Runtime acceleration; auto-falls back to torch CrossEncoder if unavailable
candidate_multiplier = 4
max_length           = 512
batch_size           = 32

[llm]
model_name      = "gemma-4-26b-a4b-it"
base_url        = "http://<dlh-vllm-host>:9000/api/v1"
verify_ssl      = false        # UAT self-signed certs only — pydantic default is true; set true (or omit) in production
temperature     = 0.2
max_tokens      = 1024

[guardrails]
enable_pii_redaction  = true                  # regex-redact PII in-place instead of rejecting the request
pii_entities          = ["CREDIT_CARD", "IN_PAN", "IN_AADHAAR", "PHONE_NUMBER", "EMAIL_ADDRESS", "IBAN_CODE", "ACCOUNT_NUMBER"]
enable_profanity_filter = true                # better-profanity word-list check
enable_toxicity_filter  = true                # detoxify ("original") toxicity classifier
toxicity_threshold      = 0.7                 # block queries scoring >= this
grounding_threshold     = 0.15                # min lexical overlap of answer vs. retrieved context

[logging]
log_dir      = "logs"
log_filename = "app.log"
log_level    = "DEBUG"         # use INFO in production
```

### `.env` — secrets (never commit)

```bash
# Required
RAG__ELASTICSEARCH_API_KEY=<your-elasticsearch-api-key>
LLM__API_KEY=EMPTY            # use EMPTY for unauthenticated DLH endpoints

# Optional overrides
RAG__ELASTICSEARCH_HOST=https://prod-es-host:9201/
RAG__VERIFY_CERTS=true
LLM__BASE_URL=http://<dlh-vllm-host>:9000/api/v1
LLM__VERIFY_SSL=true
LLM__USE_MAX_COMPLETION_TOKENS=false   # some self-hosted vLLM builds only accept legacy max_tokens
API__CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS=["https://yono.onlinesbi.com"]
LOGGING__LOG_LEVEL=INFO
```

All settings follow the `SECTION__FIELD` naming convention — double underscores map to nested Pydantic model fields.

**Security-relevant defaults:**
- `RAG__VERIFY_CERTS` and `LLM__VERIFY_SSL` both **default to `true`**. Only set them to `false` for a UAT environment with self-signed certificates — never in production.
- CORS no longer accepts the invalid `allow_origins=["*"]` + `allow_credentials=True` combination. Origins are configurable via `API__CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS` (a list); credentials are only enabled once at least one origin is explicitly configured.
- `/api/v1/admin/ingest` and `/api/v1/chat` have **no application-level authentication by design** — they must be restricted at the network/gateway layer (internal-only ingress, VPN, or a reverse-proxy auth rule) before the service is exposed. See [Production Deployment](#production-deployment).

---

## API Endpoints

| Method | Path | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `GET` | `/health` | Real readiness check — returns `200 {"status":"ok"}` only when the embedding, LLM, and retrieval services are all initialised; otherwise `503 {"status":"unavailable","not_ready":[...]}` |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/chat` | Submit a banking question — returns RAG answer + sources. No application-level auth — restrict at the network/gateway layer |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/admin/ingest` | Re-ingest knowledge base from CSV into Elasticsearch. Defaults to `recreate=false` (appends/updates existing index); pass `?recreate=true` to explicitly delete-and-recreate the index. No application-level auth — restrict at the network/gateway layer |
| `GET` | `/` | Serves the frontend SPA (`index.html`) — mounted **after** all API routes so it never shadows `/health` or `/api/v1/*` |
| `GET` | `/docs` | Swagger UI (interactive API explorer) |
| `GET` | `/redoc` | ReDoc API documentation |

### Chat — quick example

```bash
curl -X POST http://localhost:8000/api/v1/chat \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"query": "How do I temporarily block my debit card?", "top_k": 5}'
```

```json
{
  "query": "How do I temporarily block my debit card?",
  "answer": "You can temporarily block your ATM/Debit card through the YONO app under Service Requests...",
  "category": "ATM/Debit Card Service",
  "doc_type": "Main Category",
  "score": 0.9477,
  "sources": [...],
  "session_id": null
}
```

---

## Production Deployment

```bash
# Gunicorn + Uvicorn workers (recommended for production)
gunicorn dlh_ai.genai.application.backend.main:app \
    -k uvicorn.workers.UvicornWorker \
    --workers 4 \
    --bind 0.0.0.0:8000 \
    --timeout 120
```

See [`docs/deployment.md`](docs/md_files/deployment.md) for the complete guide including systemd unit, Nginx reverse proxy, health probes, and log rotation.

### Network-layer access control (required)

`/api/v1/admin/ingest` and `/api/v1/chat` are deliberately shipped **without application-level
authentication** — there is no auth code to bypass or misconfigure at that layer. Both endpoints
must instead be protected upstream, before traffic ever reaches the FastAPI process:

- Restrict `/api/v1/admin/ingest` to an internal-only ingress path, an SBI intranet/VPN network
  segment, or a reverse-proxy rule (e.g. Nginx `allow`/`deny`, an API gateway auth policy) that
  only admin operators can reach.
- Restrict `/api/v1/chat` to the intended customer-facing network path per your deployment
  topology (YONO app backend, internal load balancer, etc.).
- Use the container launch path (`Launcher.py` + `ui.py`, see [Container / CML Launch](#container--cml-launch-launcherpy--uipy)) or Gunicorn/Nginx as described above — never expose the bare `uvicorn`
  dev server directly to an untrusted network.

### Container / CML launch (`Launcher.py` + `ui.py`)

For CML/container deployments, `src/dlh_ai/genai/application/Launcher.py` is the process entry
point. It starts two subprocesses and keeps both alive until interrupted:

1. The FastAPI backend, via `uvicorn dlh_ai.genai.application.backend.main:app` (port from
   `CB_BACKEND_PORT`, default `8008`).
2. `ui.py` — a plain **stdlib `http.server`** chat frontend (not Gradio) that talks to the
   backend over `http://127.0.0.1:<CB_BACKEND_PORT>` (port from `CDSW_APP_PORT`, default `8100`).

```bash
python src/dlh_ai/genai/application/Launcher.py
```

## Technology Stack

| Component | Technology | Version |
|---|---|---|
| Language | Python | ≥ 3.11 |
| Web framework | FastAPI | ≥ 0.110 |
| ASGI server | Uvicorn | ≥ 0.29 |
| Process manager | Gunicorn | ≥ 21.0 |
| Configuration | pydantic-settings | ≥ 2.0 |
| Vector database | Elasticsearch | 8.x / 9.x |
| Embedding model | sentence-transformers (`all-MiniLM-L12-v2`) | ≥ 3.0 |
| LLM | Gemma-4-26b-a4b-it via DLH vLLM | — |
| LLM client | openai | ≥ 1.35 |
| HTTP client | httpx | ≥ 0.27 |
| GPU compute | torch (CUDA) | ≥ 2.3 |
| Reranker acceleration (optional) | optimum[onnxruntime] — falls back to torch `CrossEncoder` when absent | ≥ 1.20.0 |
| Guardrails — PII / profanity | better-profanity (regex PII detection is built-in, no external package) | ≥ 0.7.0 |
| Guardrails — toxicity | detoxify | ≥ 0.5.2 |
| Guardrails — pipeline composition | langchain-core (`RunnableLambda`/`RunnableParallel`) | ≥ 0.3.0 |
| Prompt engine | Jinja2 | ≥ 3.1 |
| Data processing | pandas | ≥ 2.0 |
| Numerics | numpy | ≥ 1.25 |
| HDFS | pyarrow | ≥ 14.0 |
| Logging | rich | ≥ 13.0 |
| Linting | ruff | ≥ 0.4 |
| Type checking | pyrefly | ≥ 0.1 |

`pyproject.toml`'s dependency list is reconciled with `requirements.txt` — the unmaintained
`toml` package has been removed (superseded by stdlib `tomllib` + `tomli-w`), and `torch`,
`better-profanity`, `detoxify`, and `langchain-core` are pinned with matching version ranges
across both files. The bank's runtime environment only permits `better-profanity`, `detoxify`,
and `langchain_core` for guardrails/evaluation — `presidio-analyzer`, `presidio-anonymizer`,
`nemoguardrails`, `spacy`, `ragas`, and `datasets` are no longer dependencies anywhere in the
project.

---

## Reliability & Frontend Hardening

Recent production-readiness fixes beyond routing/auth/TLS/CORS (above):

- **LLM 429 handling** — `LlmService` now retries `429 Too Many Requests` responses with
  backoff instead of treating them as fatal, honouring a server-sent `Retry-After` header
  when present. Its retry loop's error message also genuinely reports the `last_exc` that
  caused the final failure (previously unused).
- **Embedding FP16 fallback** — `EmbeddingService`'s `model.half()` cast to FP16 is wrapped
  in a `try`/`except RuntimeError`, falling back to FP32 automatically on GPUs/drivers that
  don't support the half-precision path, instead of crashing startup.
- **Guardrail injection heuristic** — `_guardrails.py` no longer flags legitimate banking
  answers as blocked based on a substring match on words like "cannot" or "I'm sorry" (which
  show up in normal FAQ text, e.g. "We're sorry for the inconvenience"). It now matches
  distinctive, multi-word refusal phrases via regex, greatly reducing false positives.
- **Frontend (`index.html` / `app.js`)**:
  - `Content-Security-Policy` meta tag (`default-src 'self'; script-src 'self'; ...`).
  - Inline `<script>` extracted to an external same-origin `app.js` (required for
    `script-src 'self'` to work without `'unsafe-inline'`).
  - Chat textarea now has `maxlength="2000"`, matching the backend's `ChatRequest.query`
    `max_length=2000` validation.
  - Session IDs generated with `crypto.randomUUID()` instead of `Math.random()`.
  - `esc()` now also escapes `'` and backtick, not just `<`/`>`/`&`/`"`.
  - Backend error bodies are no longer rendered verbatim to the user — a generic message
    is shown while the real error is only logged to the browser console.
  - The `Blob` URL created by `exportChat()` is revoked (`URL.revokeObjectURL`) after use.
- **`ui.py` / `Launcher.py`** (the stdlib `http.server` container UI and its process
  launcher — see [Container / CML Launch](#container--cml-launch-launcherpy--uipy)):
  `print()` calls replaced with `logging`; the bare `except:` in `ui.py`'s
  `_check_backend()` now logs the caught exception instead of silently swallowing it;
  `ui.py`'s `escHtml()` now escapes `'`/backtick like the SPA's `esc()`; session IDs use
  `crypto.randomUUID()`; stale "Gradio" references in `Launcher.py`'s docstrings/comments
  were corrected — `ui.py` is a plain stdlib `http.server` app, not Gradio.

---

## Coding Standards

All Python source files follow:

1. **NumPy-style docstrings** on all public modules, classes, functions, and methods
2. **Full type hints** — modern syntax (`list[str]`, `str | None`), no `Any`
3. **Relative imports** within the same package
4. **Pyrefly** static type checking compatibility
5. **Ruff** linting — line-length 120, isort, E/W/F/I/UP/B/C4/PLC/PLW rules
6. **Detailed exception handling** — specific types, `raise ... from exc` chaining, meaningful messages, no secrets in error text
7. **`logging`** throughout — no `print()` calls
8. **No hardcoded secrets** — all credentials via `.env` / environment variables

# Path to the SentenceTransformer snapshot directory on HDFS.
# The path must point to the resolved snapshot directory, not the cache root.
#
# Available models (uncomment the one to activate; keep the others commented):
#
#   Option 1: SentenceTransformers all-MiniLM-L12-v2  (dim=384, lightweight)

# Elasticsearch per-request socket timeout in seconds.

# Set to true in production to enable TLS certificate verification.
# Leave false for UAT environments with self-signed certificates.
verify_certs = false

# Size of the Elasticsearch connection pool per node.
# Increase for high-concurrency deployments (10M+ users).
connections_per_node = 20

# Default number of knowledge-base documents to retrieve per query.
default_top_k = 5

# Maximum number of documents the user can request per query.
max_top_k = 20

# URL template for a "view source row" link shown once in every chat reply,
# built from the top-ranked source's Sno. "{sno}" is substituted at runtime
# with that document's serial number, e.g.:
#   source_table_url_template = "https://example.com/knowledge-base/row/{sno}"
source_table_url_template = ""

# When true, chat replies include the source-table link above (only when
# source_table_url_template is also set). When false, the link is omitted
# from every reply, regardless of the template.
enable_source_table_link = false

# Reranker configuration - optional cross-encoder second-stage reranking ----
[reranker]

# When true, the vector-search candidate pool is widened by
# candidate_multiplier and re-scored by the cross-encoder model below before
# being truncated back down to the requested top_k.
# Temporarily disabled: the mxbai-rerank-large-v2 model has not yet been
# staged on HDFS at the path below. Re-enable once it has been uploaded, or
# point model_path at an already-staged model.
enabled = false

# Path to the cross-encoder reranker model snapshot directory on HDFS.
#
# Available models (uncomment the one to activate; keep the others commented):
#
#   Option 1: mixedbread-ai/mxbai-rerank-large-v2  (large, multilingual)
model_path = "/dev/aiml/models/mixedbread-ai/mxbai-rerank-large-v2/"
#
#   Option 2: cross-encoder/ms-marco-MiniLM-L-6-v2  (lightweight, English-only)
# model_path = "cross-encoder/ms-marco-MiniLM-L-6-v2"

# Attempt to load and run the model via ONNX Runtime (optimum) for faster
# inference, falling back automatically to the torch CrossEncoder backend if
# optimum/onnxruntime are not installed or the ONNX load fails.
use_onnx = true

# Retrieve top_k * candidate_multiplier candidates from vector search before
# reranking, then truncate back down to top_k.
candidate_multiplier = 4

# Max token length per (query, passage) pair passed to the reranker.
max_length = 512

# Pairs scored per reranker forward pass.
batch_size = 32

# Multi-turn chat history configuration - optional, Elasticsearch-backed -----
[chat_history]

# When true, each session is given a server-generated 12-digit ID, prior
# turns (query + answer) for that session are loaded and fed into the LLM
# prompt as conversational memory, and the new turn is saved after every
# reply. When false, every query is answered statelessly (current behaviour)
# and no chat-history Elasticsearch calls are made.
# Temporarily disabled: elasticsearch_host/index_name below are placeholders,
# a dedicated chat-history cluster/index has not been provisioned yet.
# Re-enable once real values are set.
enabled = false

# Placeholder - point at a dedicated chat-history Elasticsearch cluster once
# provisioned. Intentionally NOT the same cluster/index as [rag] above.
elasticsearch_host = "https://localhost:9200/"

# Placeholder index/db name for the chat-history store.
index_name = "yono_chat_history_dummy"

# Maximum number of prior turns (query+answer pairs) loaded per session and
# injected into the LLM prompt.
max_history_turns = 5

# LLM configuration - DLH self-hosted OpenAI-compatible endpoint -------------
[llm]

# Model identifier sent in every completion request.
# Override via environment: LLM__MODEL_NAME=google/gemma-4-31b-it
model_name = "google/gemma-4-31b-it"

# Base URL for the DLH OpenAI-compatible inference server.
# Override via environment: LLM__BASE_URL=http://10.190.236.15:9000/api/v1
base_url = "http://10.190.236.15:9000/api/v1"

# Set to true in production to enable TLS certificate verification.
# Override via environment: LLM__VERIFY_SSL=true
verify_ssl = false

# NOTE: api_key must NOT appear here.
#       Set it via LLM__API_KEY in the .env file or environment.

# Generation parameters -------------------------------------------------------

# Sampling temperature in [0.0, 2.0]. Lower = more deterministic.
temperature = 0.2

# Maximum number of tokens to generate per response.
max_tokens = 1024

# HTTP request timeout per LLM call in seconds.
request_timeout = 60.0

# Guardrails configuration - heuristic PII / profanity / toxicity / topic checks
# No field below triggers an LLM call. Every check is a regex/lexical
# heuristic or a local classifier (detoxify).
[guardrails]

# When true, PII found in user queries and LLM answers is redacted in-place.
# When false, a PIIDetectedError is raised on the user query and the request
# is rejected.
enable_pii_redaction = true

# PII entity types to detect via regex.  Covers Indian banking identifiers.
pii_entities = [
    "CREDIT_CARD",
    "IN_PAN",
    "IN_AADHAAR",
    "PHONE_NUMBER",
    "EMAIL_ADDRESS",
    "IBAN_CODE",
    "ACCOUNT_NUMBER",
]

# When true, queries containing profane language are blocked (better-profanity).
enable_profanity_filter = true

# When true, queries are scored by the detoxify toxicity classifier and
# blocked at or above toxicity_threshold.
# Temporarily disabled: detoxify's checkpoint (.ckpt) has not yet been
# staged on HDFS (see detoxify_checkpoint_path below) and this environment
# has no outbound internet access to fetch it directly. Re-enable once the
# checkpoint has been uploaded to the configured HDFS path.
enable_toxicity_filter = false

# Toxicity score (0.0-1.0) at or above which a query is blocked.
toxicity_threshold = 0.7

# Minimum fraction (0.0-1.0) of the answer's content words that must overlap
# with the retrieved context for the answer to be considered grounded.
grounding_threshold = 0.15

# HDFS path to a pre-staged detoxify "original" checkpoint (.ckpt file).
# REQUIRED in the bank network: Detoxify("original") otherwise tries to
# download this file from GitHub at startup, which fails because the bank
# network has no outbound internet access (DNS/connection error).
#
# One-time setup (on any machine with internet access):
#   1. Download the checkpoint:
#      https://github.com/unitaryai/detoxify/releases/download/v0.1-alpha/toxic_original-c1212f89.ckpt
#   2. Upload it to HDFS, e.g.:
#      hdfs dfs -put toxic_original-c1212f89.ckpt /dev/aiml/models/detoxify/toxic_original.ckpt
#   3. Set the path below to match.
#
# Leave unset only in environments with outbound internet access (e.g. local
# development). enable_toxicity_filter can also be set to false to skip
# loading the model entirely.
detoxify_checkpoint_path = "/dev/aiml/models/detoxify/toxic_original.ckpt"

# SIT / Testing thresholds - from-scratch heuristic RAG evaluation -----------
# (see dlh_ai.genai.rag._evaluation, no LLM-as-judge, no ragas/datasets)
[testing]

# Path to the curated golden Q&A dataset (CSV: question, ground_truth columns).
golden_qa_dataset_path = "tests/data/golden_qa.csv"

# Minimum acceptable mean faithfulness score (claims grounded in context).
faithfulness_threshold = 0.8

# Minimum acceptable mean answer-relevance score (query/answer embedding
# cosine similarity).
answer_relevancy_threshold = 0.75

# Minimum acceptable mean context-precision score.
context_precision_threshold = 0.7

# Minimum acceptable mean context-recall score.
context_recall_threshold = 0.7

# Logging configuration -------------------------------------------------------
[logging]

# Directory (relative to the project root) where rotating log files are written.
log_dir = "logs"

# Log file name.
log_filename = "app.log"

# Logging level for file handler: DEBUG | INFO | WARNING | ERROR | CRITICAL
log_level = "DEBUG"



####################################################################################################
FILE: src\dlh_ai\__init__.py
####################################################################################################


import logging
import os
from logging import Formatter, getLogger
from rich.console import Console
from rich.logging import RichHandler

#     MetricRecord,
# )




LOG_DIR = "logs"
LOG_FILENAME = "app.log"
LOG_FORMAT_CONSOLE = "%(message)s"
LOG_FORMAT_FILE = "%(asctime)s | %(levelname)s | %(name)s | %(module)s:%(filename)s:%(lineno)d | %(funcName)s | %(message)s"
DATE_FORMAT = "%d-%b-%y %H:%M:%S"
LOG_LEVEL = logging.DEBUG


"""Configures and returns the logger for the dc_nbo library."""
logger = getLogger("dlh_ai")
logger.setLevel(LOG_LEVEL)

console_handler = RichHandler(
    markup=True,
    show_path=False,
    console=Console(),
    locals_max_string=150,
)
console_handler.setFormatter(Formatter(LOG_FORMAT_CONSOLE, datefmt=DATE_FORMAT))
console_handler.flush()  # Ensure immediate output
logger.addHandler(console_handler)

os.makedirs(LOG_DIR, exist_ok=True)

file_handler = logging.FileHandler(
    filename=os.path.join(LOG_DIR, LOG_FILENAME),
    mode="a",  # Append mode
    encoding="utf8",
)
file_handler.setFormatter(Formatter(LOG_FORMAT_FILE))
logger.addHandler(file_handler)


####################################################################################################
FILE: src\dlh_ai\config.py
####################################################################################################

"""
Application configuration module.

Loads settings from (in priority order):
1. Environment variables (highest priority), e.g.  RAG__ELASTICSEARCH_HOST=...
2. ``.env`` file entries using the same nested format, e.g. RAG__ELASTICSEARCH_HOST=...
3. ``src/config.toml`` defaults (lowest priority).
4. Direct values passed to :class:`Settings` constructor.

All secrets (API keys, passwords) must be supplied via environment variables
or the ``.env`` file, never hardcoded in source or committed to version control.

Pydantic models
---------------
* :class:`Common`: project-level metadata.
* :class:`Data`: data layer configuration.
* :class:`Training`: model training configuration.
* :class:`Inference`: inference configuration.
* :class:`RagSettings`: full RAG pipeline configuration.
* :class:`LlmSettings`: LLM configuration for the DLH self-hosted endpoint.
* :class:`LoggingSettings`: log directory and level.
* :class:`Settings`: root settings aggregating all sub-models.

Example environment overrides
------------------------------
.. code-block:: bash

    # Elasticsearch API key: SECRET
    RAG__ELASTICSEARCH_API_KEY=<base64-api-key>

    # DLH model and endpoint: non-secrets (can also live in config.toml)
    LLM__MODEL_NAME=gemma-4-26b-a4b-it
    LLM__BASE_URL=http://<dlh-vllm-host>:9000/api/v1

    # DLH API key: "EMPTY" for unauthenticated endpoints
    LLM__API_KEY=EMPTY

    # Common project override
    COMMON__PROJECT_ID=yono-chatbot-prod
"""

from functools import lru_cache
from pathlib import Path

from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
from pydantic_settings import (
    BaseSettings,
    PydanticBaseSettingsSource,
    SettingsConfigDict,
    TomlConfigSettingsSource,
)


# Sub-models


class Common(BaseModel):
    """Project-level metadata shared across all pipeline components.

    Attributes
    ----------
    project_id : str
        CML project identifier.
    connection_name : str
        Hive / data warehouse connection name.
    mlflow_experiment_name : str
        MLflow experiment name for run tracking.
    """

    project_id: str
    connection_name: str
    mlflow_experiment_name: str


class Data(BaseModel):
    """Data layer configuration placeholder.

    Attributes
    ----------
    dummy : str
        Placeholder field, replace with actual data configuration.
    """

    dummy: str = ""


class Training(BaseModel):
    """Model training configuration placeholder.

    Attributes
    ----------
    dummy : str
        Placeholder field, replace with actual training configuration.
    """

    dummy: str = ""


class Inference(BaseModel):
    """Model inference configuration placeholder.

    Attributes
    ----------
    dummy : str
        Placeholder field, replace with actual inference configuration.
    """

    dummy: str = ""


class RagSettings(BaseModel):
    """Full RAG pipeline configuration for the YONO Business Chatbot.

    All fields have sensible defaults from ``config.toml``. Secrets such
    as ``elasticsearch_api_key`` must be supplied via environment variable
    or ``.env``, they are never stored in ``config.toml``.

    Attributes
    ----------
    elasticsearch_host : str
        Elasticsearch cluster URL including scheme and port.
    elasticsearch_api_key : str
        API key for authenticating with Elasticsearch.
        Must be supplied via ``RAG__ELASTICSEARCH_API_KEY`` env variable.
    index_name : str
        Elasticsearch index that holds the YONO knowledge-base documents.
    hdfs_model_path : str
        Absolute HDFS path to the SentenceTransformer model snapshot.
    embedding_dimension : int
        Output dimension of the embedding model. Default is 384.
    csv_file_path : str
        Path to the YONO FAQ knowledge-base CSV file.
    batch_size : int
        Rows per Elasticsearch bulk-ingestion request. Default is 100.
    request_timeout : int
        Elasticsearch per-request socket timeout in seconds. Default is 30.
    max_retries : int
        Number of automatic retries on transient Elasticsearch failures.
    retry_on_timeout : bool
        Whether to retry requests that time out. Default is True.
    verify_certs : bool
        Enable TLS certificate verification. Default is True. Only set to
        False via ``RAG__VERIFY_CERTS=false`` in a UAT environment that
        uses self-signed certificates, never in production.
    connections_per_node : int
        Elasticsearch connection pool size per node. Default is 100.
    default_top_k : int
        Default number of knowledge-base documents to retrieve. Default is 5.
    max_top_k : int
        Maximum ``top_k`` a user can request. Default is 20.
    source_table_url_template : str
        URL template for a "view source row" link shown once per chat reply,
        built from the top-ranked source's ``sno``. The literal ``"{sno}"``
        placeholder is substituted at runtime. Empty string disables the link.
    enable_source_table_link : bool
        Whether to include the source-table link in chat replies. Default is
        False. Has no effect if ``source_table_url_template`` is empty.
    thread_pool_size : int
        Number of worker threads in the FastAPI blocking-call thread pool.
        Rule of thumb: set to (expected_concurrent_users / avg_llm_latency_s).
        Example: 1000 users × 2 s avg LLM → 32 threads keeps queue short.
        Default is 32.
    embedding_fp16 : bool
        Cast the embedding model to FP16 on CUDA to halve VRAM and double
        throughput on Tensor-Core GPUs (Volta+). Default is True.
    embedding_gpu_batch_size : int
        Texts per GPU forward pass during bulk encoding. Larger = more VRAM,
        higher throughput. Default is 256.
    embedding_dynamic_batching : bool
        Coalesce concurrent single-query encodes into GPU-sized batches.
        Strongly recommended for high-QPS. Default is True.
    embedding_dynamic_batch_max_size : int
        Maximum queries per GPU dispatch in the dynamic batcher. Default is 64.
    """

    elasticsearch_host: str = "https://localhost:9200/"
    elasticsearch_api_key: str = Field(
        default="",
        description="Elasticsearch API key, supply via RAG__ELASTICSEARCH_API_KEY env var.",
    )
    index_name: str = "yono_business_knowledge_base"
    hdfs_model_path: str = (
        "/path/to/sentence-transformers/model/snapshot"
    )
    embedding_dimension: int = 384
    csv_file_path: str = "yono_business_knowledge_base_fill_category.csv"
    batch_size: int = 100
    request_timeout: int = 30
    max_retries: int = 3
    retry_on_timeout: bool = True
    verify_certs: bool = True
    connections_per_node: int = 100
    default_top_k: int = 5
    max_top_k: int = 20
    source_table_url_template: str = ""
    enable_source_table_link: bool = False
    # Threading & GPU
    thread_pool_size: int = 32
    embedding_fp16: bool = True
    embedding_gpu_batch_size: int = 256
    embedding_dynamic_batching: bool = True
    embedding_dynamic_batch_max_size: int = 64


class RerankerSettings(BaseModel):
    """Optional cross-encoder reranker configuration.

    Mirrors the ``[reranker]`` section of ``config.toml``. Wired into
    :class:`~dlh_ai.genai.rag.RerankerService` and, when enabled, into
    :class:`~dlh_ai.genai.rag.RetrievalService` at application startup.

    Attributes
    ----------
    enabled : bool
        When ``False``, the reranker model is never downloaded or loaded
        and retrieval falls back to raw vector-search ranking at zero
        extra cost. Default is ``False``.
    model_path : str
        HDFS path (or a HuggingFace Hub id, e.g. for local development
        with internet access) to the cross-encoder reranker model.
        Required when ``enabled=True``.
    use_onnx : bool
        Attempt to load and run the model via ONNX Runtime (``optimum``)
        for faster inference, falling back automatically to the torch
        ``CrossEncoder`` backend if ``optimum``/``onnxruntime`` are not
        installed or the ONNX load fails. Default is ``True``.
    candidate_multiplier : int
        Retrieve ``top_k * candidate_multiplier`` candidates from vector
        search before reranking, then truncate back down to ``top_k``.
        Default is 4.
    max_length : int
        Max token length per (query, passage) pair passed to the
        reranker. Default is 512.
    batch_size : int
        Pairs scored per reranker forward pass. Default is 32.
    """

    enabled: bool = False
    model_path: str = ""
    use_onnx: bool = True
    candidate_multiplier: int = 4
    max_length: int = 512
    batch_size: int = 32


class ChatHistorySettings(BaseModel):
    """Optional multi-turn chat history configuration.

    Mirrors the ``[chat_history]`` section of ``config.toml``. Wired into
    :class:`~dlh_ai.genai.rag.ChatHistoryService` and, when enabled, into
    :class:`~dlh_ai.genai.rag.RetrievalService` at application startup.

    Attributes
    ----------
    enabled : bool
        Master toggle for multi-turn chat. When ``False``, every query is
        answered statelessly (no history loaded or saved) and no
        chat-history Elasticsearch calls are made, though a server-side
        12-digit session ID is still generated for every request without
        one. Default is ``False``.
    elasticsearch_host : str
        Elasticsearch URL for the *dedicated* chat-history cluster.
        Intentionally independent of ``[rag].elasticsearch_host``.
        Placeholder value until a real cluster is provisioned.
    elasticsearch_api_key : str
        API key for the chat-history Elasticsearch cluster. Secret, should
        be supplied via ``CHAT_HISTORY__ELASTICSEARCH_API_KEY`` (env or
        ``.env``), never committed to ``config.toml``.
    index_name : str
        Elasticsearch index/db name that stores chat turns. Placeholder
        value until a real index is provisioned.
    max_history_turns : int
        Maximum number of prior turns (query + answer pairs) loaded per
        session and injected into the LLM prompt. Default is 5.
    """

    enabled: bool = False
    elasticsearch_host: str = "https://localhost:9200/"
    elasticsearch_api_key: str = Field(
        default="", description="Secret - supply via environment variable."
    )
    index_name: str = "yono_chat_history_dummy"
    max_history_turns: int = 5


class LlmSettings(BaseModel):
    """LLM configuration for the DLH self-hosted OpenAI-compatible endpoint.

    Secrets (API keys) must be supplied via environment variables or the
    ``.env`` file. Non-sensitive fields (URL, model name) can also live
    in ``config.toml``.

    Attributes
    ----------
    model_name : str
        Model identifier sent in every completion request.
        Default is ``"gemma-4-26b-a4b-it"``.
    base_url : str
        Base URL for the DLH vLLM OpenAI-compatible inference server.
        Default is ``"http://10.190.236.15:9000/api/v1"``.
    api_key : str
        API key for the DLH endpoint. Use ``"EMPTY"`` for unauthenticated
        endpoints. Must be supplied via ``LLM__API_KEY`` env variable.
    verify_ssl : bool
        Whether to verify the TLS certificate of the endpoint.
        Default is ``True``. Only set to False via ``LLM__VERIFY_SSL=false``
        for a UAT environment with self-signed certificates.
    temperature : float
        Sampling temperature. Range [0.0, 2.0]. Default is 0.2.
    max_tokens : int
        Maximum tokens in the generated response. Default is 1024.
    request_timeout : float
        HTTP timeout in seconds per LLM call. Default is 60.0.
    connection_pool_size : int
        Number of keep-alive HTTP connections to the vLLM server.
        Should be >= thread_pool_size. Default is 100.
    max_retries : int
        Retries on transient LLM errors (5xx / connection reset / 429).
        Default is 3.
    use_max_completion_tokens : bool
        Whether to send the ``max_completion_tokens`` parameter (newer
        OpenAI Chat Completions spec) instead of the legacy ``max_tokens``
        parameter. Some self-hosted vLLM builds only understand the legacy
        name; set to ``False`` via ``LLM__USE_MAX_COMPLETION_TOKENS=false``
        if the server rejects ``max_completion_tokens``. Default is False
        (legacy ``max_tokens``, the widest-compatible option for vLLM).
    """

    model_name: str = "gemma-4-26b-a4b-it"
    base_url: str = "http://localhost:9000/api/v1"
    api_key: str = Field(
        default="EMPTY",
        description=(
            "DLH API key, use 'EMPTY' for unauthenticated endpoints. "
            "Supply via LLM__API_KEY env variable."
        ),
    )
    verify_ssl: bool = True
    temperature: float = 0.2
    max_tokens: int = 1024
    request_timeout: float = 60.0
    connection_pool_size: int = 100
    max_retries: int = 3
    use_max_completion_tokens: bool = False


class LoggingSettings(BaseModel):
    """Logging infrastructure configuration.

    Attributes
    ----------
    log_dir : str
        Directory (relative to project root) for rotating log files.
    log_filename : str
        Name of the log file. Default is ``"app.log"``.
    log_level : str
        Logging level for the file handler.
        One of DEBUG, INFO, WARNING, ERROR, CRITICAL. Default is DEBUG.
    """

    log_dir: str = "logs"
    log_filename: str = "app.log"
    log_level: str = "DEBUG"


class GuardrailSettings(BaseModel):
    """Guardrail configuration: heuristic PII/profanity/toxicity/topic checks.

    Mirrors the ``[guardrails]`` section of ``config.toml``. Wired into
    :class:`~dlh_ai.genai.rag.GuardrailService` at application startup. No
    field here triggers an LLM call, every check is a pure regex/lexical
    heuristic or a local classifier (``detoxify``).

    Attributes
    ----------
    enable_pii_redaction : bool
        Whether detected PII entities should be redacted in-place in both
        the user query and the LLM answer before it reaches the caller.
        When False, a ``PIIDetectedError`` is raised on the user query
        instead. Default is True.
    pii_entities : list[str]
        Regex-based PII entity types to detect and redact, e.g.
        ``"CREDIT_CARD"``, ``"IN_PAN"``, ``"IN_AADHAAR"``, ``"PHONE_NUMBER"``,
        ``"EMAIL_ADDRESS"``, ``"IBAN_CODE"``, ``"ACCOUNT_NUMBER"``.
    enable_profanity_filter : bool
        Whether to block queries containing profane language (via
        ``better-profanity``). Default is True.
    enable_toxicity_filter : bool
        Whether to score queries with the ``detoxify`` toxicity classifier
        and block those at or above ``toxicity_threshold``. Default is True.
    toxicity_threshold : float
        Toxicity score (0-1) at or above which a query is blocked.
        Default is 0.7.
    grounding_threshold : float
        Minimum fraction (0-1) of the answer's content words that must
        overlap with the retrieved context for the answer to be considered
        grounded (not hallucinated). Default is 0.15.
    detoxify_checkpoint_path : str, optional
        HDFS path to a pre-staged ``detoxify`` "original" checkpoint
        (``.ckpt`` file). Required in the bank network, which has no
        outbound internet access. ``detoxify`` otherwise tries to
        download this file from GitHub at startup and fails. Leave unset
        only in environments with internet access. Default is ``None``.
    """

    enable_pii_redaction: bool = True
    pii_entities: list[str] = Field(
        default_factory=lambda: [
            "CREDIT_CARD",
            "IN_PAN",
            "IN_AADHAAR",
            "PHONE_NUMBER",
            "EMAIL_ADDRESS",
            "IBAN_CODE",
            "ACCOUNT_NUMBER",
        ]
    )
    enable_profanity_filter: bool = True
    enable_toxicity_filter: bool = True
    toxicity_threshold: float = 0.7
    grounding_threshold: float = 0.15
    detoxify_checkpoint_path: str | None = None


class TestingSettings(BaseModel):
    """SIT / offline RAG-evaluation thresholds.

    Mirrors the ``[testing]`` section of ``config.toml``. Consumed by
    ``src/jobs/evaluate_rag_job.py`` and the SIT RAG-quality tests to
    gate on the from-scratch heuristic metrics in
    :mod:`dlh_ai.genai.rag._evaluation`.

    Attributes
    ----------
    golden_qa_dataset_path : str
        Path (relative to project root) to the curated golden Q&A CSV
        used for RAG evaluation. Must have ``question`` and
        ``ground_truth`` columns.
    faithfulness_threshold : float
        Minimum acceptable mean faithfulness score (claims grounded in
        retrieved context). Default is 0.8.
    answer_relevancy_threshold : float
        Minimum acceptable mean answer-relevance score (query/answer
        embedding cosine similarity). Default is 0.75.
    context_precision_threshold : float
        Minimum acceptable mean context-precision score. Default is 0.7.
    context_recall_threshold : float
        Minimum acceptable mean context-recall score. Default is 0.7.
    """

    golden_qa_dataset_path: str = "tests/data/golden_qa.csv"
    faithfulness_threshold: float = 0.8
    answer_relevancy_threshold: float = 0.75
    context_precision_threshold: float = 0.7
    context_recall_threshold: float = 0.7


class ApiSettings(BaseModel):
    """HTTP-layer configuration: CORS and related browser-facing policy.

    Attributes
    ----------
    cors_allowed_origins : list[str]
        Explicit list of origins allowed to call the API with credentials.
        A wildcard ``"*"`` is invalid when ``allow_credentials=True`` (per
        the Fetch/CORS spec) and must never be used together with
        credentialed requests. Default is an empty list, no cross-origin
        browser access until explicitly configured for the deployment's
        real frontend origin(s), e.g. ``["https://yono.onlinesbi.com"]``.
    """

    cors_allowed_origins: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)


# Root settings model


class Settings(BaseSettings):
    """Application settings loaded from env vars, .env, and config.toml.

    Nested fields can be overridden from environment variables by using the
    ``env_nested_delimiter="__"`` separator.

    Environment variable examples
    -----------------------------
    * ``COMMON__PROJECT_ID=yono-chatbot-prod``
    * ``RAG__ELASTICSEARCH_HOST=https://host:9201``
    * ``RAG__ELASTICSEARCH_API_KEY=<secret-key>``   ← required, set in .env
    * ``RAG__VERIFY_CERTS=true``
    * ``LLM__API_KEY=EMPTY``                        ← required, set in .env
    * ``LLM__BASE_URL=http://<dlh-vllm-host>:9000/api/v1``
    * ``LLM__MODEL_NAME=gemma-4-26b-a4b-it``
    * ``LOGGING__LOG_LEVEL=INFO``

    Priority order (highest → lowest)
    ----------------------------------
    1. Environment variables
    2. ``.env`` file
    3. ``config.toml`` defaults
    4. Constructor arguments
    """

    common: Common
    data: Data
    training: Training
    inference: Inference
    rag: RagSettings = Field(default_factory=RagSettings)
    reranker: RerankerSettings = Field(default_factory=RerankerSettings)
    chat_history: ChatHistorySettings = Field(default_factory=ChatHistorySettings)
    llm: LlmSettings = Field(default_factory=LlmSettings)
    logging: LoggingSettings = Field(default_factory=LoggingSettings)
    guardrails: GuardrailSettings = Field(default_factory=GuardrailSettings)
    testing: TestingSettings = Field(default_factory=TestingSettings)
    api: ApiSettings = Field(default_factory=ApiSettings)

    model_config = SettingsConfigDict(
        env_nested_delimiter="__",
        env_file=".env",
        env_file_encoding="utf-8",
    )

    @classmethod
    def settings_customise_sources(
        cls,
        settings_cls: type[BaseSettings],
        init_settings: PydanticBaseSettingsSource,
        env_settings: PydanticBaseSettingsSource,
        dotenv_settings: PydanticBaseSettingsSource,
        file_secret_settings: PydanticBaseSettingsSource,
    ) -> tuple[PydanticBaseSettingsSource, ...]:
        """Define settings source priority.

        Returns
        -------
        tuple[PydanticBaseSettingsSource, ...]
            Ordered tuple of sources from highest to lowest priority.
        """
        return (
            env_settings,           # 1st priority: environment variables
            dotenv_settings,        # 2nd priority: .env file
            TomlConfigSettingsSource(settings_cls),  # 3rd priority: config.toml
            init_settings,          # 4th priority: constructor kwargs
        )

    def save_as_toml(self) -> None:
        """Persist current settings back to the TOML configuration file.

        Uses ``tomli-w`` (Python 3.11+) for serialisation.  ``tomli-w`` is the
        write companion to the stdlib ``tomllib`` reader and replaces the
        unmaintained ``toml`` package (last release 2021, deprecated as of
        Python 3.11 which ships ``tomllib`` in stdlib).

        Raises
        ------
        KeyError
            Raised when ``toml_file`` is not set in ``model_config``.
        OSError
            Raised when the file cannot be written due to permission or
            disk-space issues.
        """
        import tomli_w  # noqa: PLC0415 (optional write-only dep)

        _file: str | Path | None = self.model_config.get("toml_file")
        if not _file:
            raise KeyError(
                "Settings.save_as_toml() requires 'toml_file' to be set in "
                "model_config before calling. Use get_settings() to initialise."
            )
        with open(_file, "wb") as fb:
            tomli_w.dump(self.model_dump(), fb)


# Helpers


def ensure_config_env(config_path: Path | str | None = None) -> Path:
    """Resolve and validate the configuration file path.

    Parameters
    ----------
    config_path : pathlib.Path or str or None, optional
        Path to ``config.toml``. When ``None``, defaults to
        ``<project_root>/src/config.toml``.

    Returns
    -------
    pathlib.Path
        Resolved absolute path to the configuration file.

    Raises
    ------
    FileNotFoundError
        Raised when the resolved path does not point to an existing file.

    Examples
    --------
    >>> path = ensure_config_env()
    >>> print(path)
    /workspace/src/config.toml
    """
    if not config_path:
        # Default: two levels up from this file → project root / src / config.toml
        p = Path(__file__).parent.parent / "config.toml"
    else:
        p = Path(config_path)

    if p.is_file():
        return p.resolve()

    raise FileNotFoundError(
        f"Configuration file not found at '{p.resolve()}'. "
        "Ensure 'src/config.toml' exists or pass an explicit path to get_settings()."
    )


@lru_cache(maxsize=8)
def get_settings(config_path: str | Path | None = None) -> Settings:
    """Load and return application settings from all configured sources.

    Cached per distinct ``config_path`` via :func:`functools.lru_cache`.
    Settings are parsed from disk once and reused for the lifetime of the
    process. Call :meth:`get_settings.cache_clear` to force a re-read
    (e.g. in tests, or after a config file change during development).

    Parameters
    ----------
    config_path : str or pathlib.Path or None, optional
        Explicit path to ``config.toml``. When ``None``, the default
        ``src/config.toml`` location is used.

    Returns
    -------
    Settings
        Fully populated settings object.

    Raises
    ------
    FileNotFoundError
        Raised when the configuration file cannot be found.
    pydantic_settings.ValidationError
        Raised when required settings fields are missing or have invalid
        values after merging all sources.

    Examples
    --------
    >>> settings = get_settings()
    >>> print(settings.rag.index_name)
    yono_business_knowledge_base

    >>> # Override the config file path
    >>> settings = get_settings("/custom/path/config.toml")
    """
    resolved: Path = ensure_config_env(config_path)

    # Register the TOML file path on the model_config so BaseSettings reads it.
    Settings.model_config["toml_file"] = resolved.as_posix()

    return Settings()

####################################################################################################
FILE: src\dlh_ai\data\_pandas_backend.py

import logging
import tempfile
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Literal

import cml.data_v1 as cmldata
import pandas as pd
from dlh_ai.utils import HDFSClient
from sqlglot import Expression, parse_one
from tenacity import (
    before_sleep_log,
    retry,
    retry_if_exception_type,
    stop_after_attempt,
    wait_exponential,
)

logger = logging.getLogger("dlh_ai")


@dataclass
class PandasEngine:
    connection_name: str = "DLH-DEV-UAT - Hive on Tez"

    def __post_init__(self):
        self.hdfs_client = HDFSClient()

    @property
    def cursor(self):
        """Property to get a database cursor for executing queries
        NOTE - This will create a new connection & manually it must be closed after use.
        """
        # TODO - check if this is needed or if we can just use self._create_connection.get_cursor()
        conn = self._create_connection()
        return conn.get_cursor()

    def read_table(self, query: str) -> pd.DataFrame:
        """Read a Iceberg table on Hive OCP into a Pandas DataFrame using provided sql query

        Parameters
        ----------
        query : str
            SQL query to read the table

        Returns
        -------
        pd.DataFrame
            Pandas DataFrame containing the data from the specified SQL table
        """
        # verifying the read query
        self._verify_sql_query(query)

        with self._create_connection() as base_conn:
            with base_conn.cursor() as cursor:
                logger.debug("Executing query to read the table")
                from impala.util import as_pandas

                cursor.execute(query)
                data = as_pandas(cursor)
                logger.info(f"Successfully read {len(data)} rows")

        # NOTE - reading from 'Hive on Tez' also add table name as prefix to column names,
        # so we remove it here for consistency with other engines
        return data.rename(
            columns={col: col.upper().split(".")[-1] for col in data.columns}
        )

    def read_csv_from_hdfs(self, path: str) -> pd.DataFrame:
        data = self.hdfs_client.read_csv_file(path)
        return data.to_table().to_pandas(
            split_blocks=True,
            self_destruct=True,  # frees Arrow buffers after conversion
            zero_copy_only=False,
        )

    def read_parquet_from_hdfs(self, path: str) -> pd.DataFrame:
        data = self.hdfs_client.read_parquet_table(path)
        return data.to_table().to_pandas(
            split_blocks=True,
            self_destruct=True,  # frees Arrow buffers after conversion
            zero_copy_only=False,
        )

    def save_table(
        self,
        df: pd.DataFrame,
        name: str,
        mode: Literal["overwrite", "append", "ignore", "error"] = "append",
        batch_size: int = 5000,
    ):
        """Function to insert data from a Pandas DataFrame into a Iceberg table

        Parameters
        ----------
        df : pd.DataFrame
            table data in the form of pandas dataframe
        name : str
            table name to write/append DEPRECATED -
        mode : Literal["overwrite", "append", "ignore", "error"], optional
            how to write data, by default "append"
        """
        if mode != "append":
            raise NotImplementedError(
                f"Mode '{mode}' not yet implemented for Pandas Engine"
            )
        # Prepare Query with Backticks
        # NOTE - splitting by '.' to backtick the schema and table separately: `db`.`table`
        if "." in name:
            db, tbl = name.split(".")
            formatted_table_name = f"`{db}`.`{tbl}`"
        else:
            formatted_table_name = f"`{name}`"

        # Backtick every column name to be safe
        col_names = ", ".join([f"`{c.split('.')[-1]}`" for c in df.columns])
        placeholders = ", ".join(["%s"] * len(df.columns))

        insert_sql_query = (
            f"INSERT INTO {formatted_table_name} ({col_names}) VALUES ({placeholders})"
        )

        no_of_batches = self._calculate_batch_size(df, batch_size)
        logger.info(
            f"Starting to load data into {name} in batches of {batch_size} with total batches: {no_of_batches}"
        )

        with self._create_connection() as base_conn:
            with base_conn.cursor() as cursor:
                try:
                    # cursor loop
                    for idx, offset in enumerate(
                        range(0, len(df), batch_size), start=1
                    ):
                        batch = df.iloc[offset : offset + batch_size]
                        logger.debug(f"inserting batch {idx}: out of {no_of_batches}")
                        cursor.executemany(
                            insert_sql_query, batch.to_dict(orient="records")
                        )
                        logger.debug(
                            f"successfully inserted batch {idx} with {len(batch)} rows."
                        )

                    logger.info(f"Successfully loaded {len(df)} rows.")
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.error(f"SQL Error: {insert_sql_query}")
                    logger.error(f"Details: {e}")
                    # FIXME - use precise exception chaining rather than generic. then use it below with `from e`
                    # raise from e
                    raise  # this is temporary until we fix the above

    def save_csv_to_hdfs(
        self,
        df: pd.DataFrame,
        path: str,
        **kwargs,
    ):
        """Save a pandas DataFrame as a CSV file to HDFS.

        Parameters
        ----------
        df : pd.DataFrame
            The DataFrame to save.
        path : str
            The HDFS path where the CSV file will be saved.
        **kwargs : dict
            Additional keyword arguments to pass to `pd.DataFrame.to_csv()`.
        """
        # REVIEW - Check if instead of local copy, we can directly use pyarrow table to write to hdfs
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp_dir:
            df.to_csv(f"{temp_dir}/data.csv", **kwargs)
            self.upload(f"{temp_dir}/data.csv", path)
        logger.info(f"saved pandas dataframe to HDFS at {path}")

    def save_parquet(self, df: pd.DataFrame, path: str, **kwargs):
        """Save a pandas DataFrame as a Parquet file to HDFS.

        Parameters
        ----------
        df : pd.DataFrame
            The DataFrame to save.
        path : str
            The HDFS path where the Parquet file will be saved.
        **kwargs : dict
            Additional keyword arguments to pass to `pd.DataFrame.to_parquet()`.
        """
        # REVIEW - Check if instead of local copy, we can directly use pyarrow table to write to hdfs
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp_dir:
            df.to_parquet(f"{temp_dir}/data.parquet", **kwargs)
            self.upload(f"{temp_dir}/data.parquet", path)
        logger.info(f"saved pandas dataframe to HDFS at {path}")

    @retry(
        stop=stop_after_attempt(5),
        wait=wait_exponential(multiplier=1, max=10),
        retry=retry_if_exception_type(Exception),
        reraise=True,
        before_sleep=before_sleep_log(logger, logging.WARNING),
    )
    def _create_connection(self):
        """Attempt to create and return the DB base connection (with retries)."""
        # TODO - we should explore connection pooling or reuse to avoid the overhead of creating
        # a new connection for every operation, but for now we will create a new connection each
        # time and rely on retries to handle transient issues.

        # if not hasattr(self, "_base_conn") or self._base_conn is None:
        self._base_conn = cmldata.get_connection(self.connection_name)
        logger.info(f"Successfully established connection: {self.connection_name}")
        return self._base_conn.get_base_connection()

    def _calculate_batch_size(self, df: pd.DataFrame, batch_size) -> int:
        """Calculate total bactches based on batch size & data length."""
        # Simple heuristic: 5000 rows per batch as a starting point
        return len(df) // batch_size + (1 if len(df) % batch_size > 0 else 0)

    # TODO - Explore optimizing connection reuse or pooling using SQLAlchemy + Impala

    def _verify_sql_query(self, query: str) -> Expression:
        return parse_one(query)
