import logging
import tempfile
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Literal

import cml.data_v1 as cmldata
import polars as pl
from sqlglot import Expression, parse_one
from tenacity import (
    before_sleep_log,
    retry,
    retry_if_exception_type,
    stop_after_attempt,
    wait_exponential,
)

logger = logging.getLogger("dlh_ai")


@dataclass
class PolarsEngine:
    connection_name: str = "DLH-DEV-UAT - Hive on Tez"

    @property
    def cursor(self):
        """Property to get a database cursor for executing queries
        NOTE - This will create a new connection & manually it must be closed after use.
        """
        # TODO - check if this is needed or if we can just use self._create_connection.get_cursor()
        conn = self._create_connection()
        return conn.get_cursor()

    def read_table(self, query: str, schema_overrides: dict | None = None) -> pl.DataFrame:
        """Read a SQL table into a Polars DataFrame using the provided connection and table name

        Parameters
        ----------
        name : str
            name of the SQL table to read, can include schema (e.g. "schema.table

        Returns
        -------
        pl.DataFrame
            Polars DataFrame containing the data from the specified SQL table
        """
        # verifying the read query
        self._verify_sql_query(query)

        with self._create_connection() as base_conn:
            with base_conn.cursor() as cursor:
                logger.debug("Executing query to read the table")
                data = pl.read_database(
                    query, connection=cursor, schema_overrides=schema_overrides
                )
                logger.info(f"Successfully read {len(data)} rows")

        # NOTE - reading from 'Hive on Tez' also add table name as prefix to column names,
        # so we remove it here for consistency with other engines
        return data.rename({col: col.upper().split(".")[-1] for col in data.columns})

    def read_csv(self, path: str) -> pl.DataFrame:
        # TODO - TO implement it use boto3 S3 route
        raise NotImplementedError(
            "Support for reading file format not yet implemented in Polars"
        )

    def read_parquet(self, path: str) -> pl.DataFrame:
        # TODO - TO implement it use boto3 S3 route
        raise NotImplementedError(
            "Support for reading file format not yet implemented in Polars"
        )

    def save_table(
        self,
        df: pl.DataFrame,
        name: str,
        mode: Literal["overwrite", "append", "ignore", "error"] = "append",
        batch_size: int = 5000,
    ):
        """Function to insert data from a Polars DataFrame into a SQL table

        Parameters
        ----------
        df : pl.DataFrame
            table data in the form of polars dataframe
        name : str
            table name to write/append DEPRECATED -
        mode : Literal["overwrite", "append", "ignore", "error"], optional
            how to write data, by default "append"
        """
        if mode != "append":
            raise NotImplementedError(
                f"Mode '{mode}' not yet implemented for Polars Engine"
            )
        # Prepare Query with Backticks
        # NOTE - splitting by '.' to backtick the schema and table separately: `db`.`table`
        if "." in name:
            db, tbl = name.split(".")
            formatted_table_name = f"`{db}`.`{tbl}`"
        else:
            formatted_table_name = f"`{name}`"

        # Backtick every column name to be safe
        col_names = ", ".join([f"`{c.split('.')[-1]}`" for c in df.columns])
        placeholders = ", ".join(["%s"] * len(df.columns))

        insert_sql_query = (
            f"INSERT INTO {formatted_table_name} ({col_names}) VALUES ({placeholders})"
        )

        no_of_batches = self._calculate_batch_size(df, batch_size)
        logger.info(
            f"Starting to load data into {name} in batches of {batch_size} with total batches: {no_of_batches}"
        )

        with self._create_connection() as base_conn:
            with base_conn.cursor() as cursor:
                try:
                    # cursor loop
                    for idx, offset in enumerate(
                        range(0, len(df), batch_size), start=1
                    ):
                        batch = df.slice(offset, batch_size)
                        logger.debug(f"inserting batch {idx}: out of {no_of_batches}")
                        cursor.executemany(insert_sql_query, batch.to_dicts())
                        logger.debug(
                            f"successfully inserted batch {idx} with {len(batch)} rows."
                        )

                    logger.info(f"Successfully loaded {len(df)} rows.")
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.error(f"SQL Error: {insert_sql_query}")
                    logger.error(f"Details: {e}")
                    # FIXME - use precise exception chaining rather than generic. then use it below with `from e`
                    # raise from e
                    raise  # this is temporary until we fix the above

    def save_csv_to_hdfs(
        self,
        df: pl.DataFrame,
        path: str,
        **kwargs,
    ):
        """Save a Polars DataFrame as a CSV file to HDFS.

        Parameters
        ----------
        df : pl.DataFrame
            The DataFrame to save.
        path : str
            The HDFS path where the CSV file will be saved.
        **kwargs : dict
            Additional keyword arguments to pass to `pd.DataFrame.to_csv()`.
        """
        # REVIEW - Check if instead of local copy, we can directly use pyarrow table to write to hdfs
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp_dir:
            df.write_csv(f"{temp_dir}/data.csv", **kwargs)
            self.upload(f"{temp_dir}/data.csv", path)
        logger.info(f"saved polars dataframe to HDFS at {path}")

    def save_parquet_to_hdfs(self, df: pl.DataFrame, path: str, **kwargs):
        """Save a Polars DataFrame as a Parquet file to HDFS.

        Parameters
        ----------
        df : pl.DataFrame
            The DataFrame to save.
        path : str
            The HDFS path where the Parquet file will be saved.
        **kwargs : dict
            Additional keyword arguments to pass to `pl.DataFrame.to_parquet()`.
        """
        # REVIEW - Check if instead of local copy, we can directly use pyarrow table to write to hdfs
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp_dir:
            df.write_parquet(f"{temp_dir}/data.parquet", **kwargs)
            self.upload(f"{temp_dir}/data.parquet", path)
        logger.info(f"saved polars dataframe to HDFS at {path}")

    @retry(
        stop=stop_after_attempt(5),
        wait=wait_exponential(multiplier=1, max=10),
        retry=retry_if_exception_type(Exception),
        reraise=True,
        before_sleep=before_sleep_log(logger, logging.WARNING),
    )
    def _create_connection(self):
        """Attempt to create and return the DB base connection (with retries)."""
        # TODO - we should explore connection pooling or reuse to avoid the overhead of creating
        # a new connection for every operation, but for now we will create a new connection each
        # time and rely on retries to handle transient issues.

        # if not hasattr(self, "_base_conn") or self._base_conn is None:
        self._base_conn = cmldata.get_connection(self.connection_name)
        logger.info(f"Successfully established connection: {self.connection_name}")
        return self._base_conn.get_base_connection()

    def _calculate_batch_size(self, df: pl.DataFrame, batch_size) -> int:
        """Calculate total bactches based on batch size & data length."""
        # Simple heuristic: 5000 rows per batch as a starting point
        return len(df) // batch_size + (1 if len(df) % batch_size > 0 else 0)

    # TODO - Explore optimizing connection reuse or pooling using SQLAlchemy + Impala

    def _verify_sql_query(self, query: str) -> Expression:
        return parse_one(query)


####################################################################################################
FILE: src\dlh_ai\data\_spark_backend.py
####################################################################################################


import logging
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Literal
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo

from pyspark.sql import SparkSession
from pyspark.sql.dataframe import DataFrame
from sqlglot import parse_one

logger = logging.getLogger("dlh_ai")


@dataclass
class SparkEngine:
    """Spark-backed data access and persistence engine.

    Parameters
    ----------
    app_name_prefix : str
        Prefix used while constructing the Spark application name during
        session initialization.
    """

    app_name_prefix: str

    def __post_init__(self):
        """Initialize the Spark session for the engine instance.

        Notes
        -----
        The Spark application name is created by combining
        ``app_name_prefix`` with the current timestamp in the
        ``Asia/Kolkata`` timezone.
        """
        # FIXME - Below logic of creating spark session logic will work with
        # Notebook session because it can access default-spark.conf but not
        # with CML Jobs
        logger.debug("creating new spark session")
        sess_name = f"{self.app_name_prefix}-{datetime.now(ZoneInfo('Asia/Kolkata')).isoformat()}"
        self.spark = SparkSession.builder.appName(sess_name).getOrCreate()
        logger.debug(f"successfully created new spark session: {sess_name}")

    def read_table(self, name: str) -> DataFrame:
        """Read a table from the Spark catalog.

        Parameters
        ----------
        name : str
            Fully qualified table name to read.

        Returns
        -------
        DataFrame
            Spark DataFrame loaded from the requested table.
        """
        return self.spark.read.table(name)

    def read_csv(self, path: str) -> DataFrame:
        """Read a CSV dataset from the provided path.

        Parameters
        ----------
        path : str
            Filesystem/Ozone path from which the CSV data should be loaded.

        Returns
        -------
        DataFrame
            Spark DataFrame created from the CSV input.
        """
        return self.spark.read.format("csv").option("path", path).load()

    def read_parquet(self, path: str) -> DataFrame:
        """Read a Parquet dataset from the provided path.

        Parameters
        ----------
        path : str
            Filesystem/Ozone path from which the Parquet data should be loaded.

        Returns
        -------
        DataFrame
            Spark DataFrame created from the Parquet input.
        """
        return self.spark.read.format("parquet").option("path", path).load()

    def save_table(
        self,
        df: DataFrame,
        name: str,
        path: str,
        mode: Literal["overwrite", "append", "ignore", "error"] = "append",
    ):
        """Persist a DataFrame as an Iceberg table.

        Parameters
        ----------
        df : DataFrame
            Spark DataFrame to be written.
        name : str
            Target table name for persistence.
        path : str
            Storage path associated with the table.
        mode : {"overwrite", "append", "ignore", "error"}, default="append"
            Write mode to use while saving the table.
        """
        # TODO - extract table path automatically
        df.write.mode(mode).format("iceberg").option("path", path).saveAsTable(name)

    def merge_table(self, df: DataFrame, merge_query: str):
        """Merge a DataFrame into a target Iceberg table using a SQL MERGE statement.

        Parameters
        ----------
        df : DataFrame
            Source Spark DataFrame that will be exposed as a temporary view for
            the merge operation.
        merge_query : str
            SQL MERGE statement used to perform the upsert or merge logic.
        """
        # parsing merge query to validate syntax & extract source name for temp view
        parsed_query = parse_one(merge_query)
        source: str = parsed_query.args.get("using").name

        # creating temp view with source table name provided in merge query
        df.createOrReplaceTempView(source)
        logger.debug(f"created {source} temp view")

        self.spark.sql(merge_query)
        logger.info(
            f"successfully merged into {parsed_query.this.db}.{parsed_query.this.name}"
        )

    def save_csv(
        self,
        df: DataFrame,
        path: str,
        mode: Literal["overwrite", "append", "ignore", "error"] = "append",
    ):
        """Write a DataFrame to CSV format.

        Parameters
        ----------
        df : DataFrame
            Spark DataFrame to be written.
        path : str
            Destination filesystem path for the CSV output.
        mode : {"overwrite", "append", "ignore", "error"}, default="append"
            Write mode to use while saving the CSV data.
        """
        df.write.mode(mode).format("csv").save(path)

    def save_parquet(
        self,
        df: DataFrame,
        path: str,
        mode: Literal["overwrite", "append", "ignore", "error"] = "append",
    ):
        """Write a DataFrame to Parquet format.

        Parameters
        ----------
        df : DataFrame
            Spark DataFrame to be written.
        path : str
            Destination filesystem path for the Parquet output.
        mode : {"overwrite", "append", "ignore", "error"}, default="append"
            Write mode to use while saving the Parquet data.
        """
        df.write.mode(mode).format("parquet").save(path)

    def close_spark_session(self):
        """Closes curent active spark session"""
        self.spark.stop()

####################################################################################################
FILE: src\dlh_ai\genai\application\Launcher.py
####################################################################################################

"""
YONO Business RAG Chatbot - Process Launcher.

Starts the FastAPI backend and the stdlib ``http.server``-based UI
(``ui.py``) as separate sub-processes and keeps them both alive until
the user presses Ctrl+C. This is the intended container entry point:
it launches both backend and frontend together as one process group.

Project layout (relative to this file)
---------------------------------------
application/
    Launcher.py          <- this file
    backend/
        main.py          <- FastAPI app  (uvicorn entry-point)
    ui.py                <- stdlib http.server frontend

Run:
    python src/dlh_ai/genai/application/Launcher.py

Environment variables
---------------------
CDSW_APP_PORT : str
    TCP port for the UI server.  Defaults to ``8100``.
    In CML, this variable is injected automatically by the platform.
CB_BACKEND_PORT : str
    TCP port for the FastAPI backend.  Defaults to ``8008``.
    Normally you do not need to override this.
"""

import os
import subprocess
import time
from pathlib import Path
def launch_yono_chatbot() -> None:
    """Launch the YONO Business FastAPI backend and the stdlib http.server UI together."""
    
    # --- 1. CONFIGURATION ---
    # Based on your project layout: src/dlh_ai/genai/application/Launcher.py
    # We set the root to the base of the source tree so imports work correctly.
    PROJECT_ROOT = "/home/cdsw/src" 
    
    # Paths to the entry-point files
    # Backend is usually started via module path 'dlh_ai.genai.application.backend.main:app'
    BACKEND_MODULE = "dlh_ai.genai.application.backend.main:app"
    UI_FILE = Path(PROJECT_ROOT) / "dlh_ai/genai/application/ui.py"

    # Ports from environment or defaults
    BACKEND_PORT: str = os.environ.get("CB_BACKEND_PORT", "8973")
    UI_PORT: str = os.environ.get("CDSW_APP_PORT", "8678")

    print("\nChecking environment...")

    # Verify UI file exists (Backend is verified by uvicorn during launch)
    if not UI_FILE.exists():
        print(f" X ERROR: UI file not found at {UI_FILE}")
        return

    # Setup Environment Variables
    env: dict[str, str] = os.environ.copy()
    env["CB_BACKEND_URL"] = f"http://127.0.0.1:{BACKEND_PORT}"
    env["CDSW_APP_PORT"] = UI_PORT

    # --- CRITICAL FIX: Add src/ to PYTHONPATH ---
    # This allows uvicorn to find the 'dlh_ai' package
    existing_pythonpath: str = env.get("PYTHONPATH", "")
    env["PYTHONPATH"] = f"{PROJECT_ROOT}:{existing_pythonpath}" if existing_pythonpath else PROJECT_ROOT

    print("Starting YONO Business Chatbot Suite...")

    backend_proc: subprocess.Popen | None = None
    ui_proc: subprocess.Popen | None = None

    try:
        # --- 2. START BACKEND ---
        print(f" Launching Backend (FastAPI) on port {BACKEND_PORT}...")
        backend_proc = subprocess.Popen(
            [
                "python", "-m", "uvicorn", 
                BACKEND_MODULE, 
                "--host", "127.0.0.1", 
                "--port", BACKEND_PORT
            ],
            env=env,
        )

        # Give the backend time to load the Embedding model from HDFS and ES client
        print(" Waiting 15 seconds for Backend to initialize (loading models)...")
        time.sleep(15)

        # Quick check if backend crashed immediately
        if backend_proc.poll() is not None:
            print("\n X ERROR: Backend crashed during startup. Check logs above.")
            return

        # --- 3. START UI SERVER ---
        print(f" Launching UI Server on port {UI_PORT}...")
        ui_proc = subprocess.Popen(
            ["python", str(UI_FILE)],
            env=env,
        )

        print("\n" + "=" * 60)
        print(" YONO BUSINESS CHATBOT - SYSTEMS STARTED")
        print(f"Backend API : http://127.0.0.1:{BACKEND_PORT}")
        print(f"API Docs    : http://127.0.0.1:{BACKEND_PORT}/docs")
        print(f"Frontend UI : http://127.0.0.1:{UI_PORT}")
        print("=" * 60)
        print("\n Use the CML-provided link for the UI port to open the chatbot.")
        print(" Press Ctrl+C to stop both servers.\n")

        # Keep the main thread alive
        while True:
            time.sleep(1)
            if backend_proc.poll() is not None:
                print("\n BACKEND CRASHED! Check terminal logs above.")
                break
            if ui_proc.poll() is not None:
                print("\n UI SERVER CRASHED! Check terminal logs above.")
                break

    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("\n XXXXX------Shutting down YONO Chatbot-----XXXXX")
    finally:
        if backend_proc is not None:
            backend_proc.terminate()
        if ui_proc is not None:
            ui_proc.terminate()
        print(" All processes stopped.")
if __name__ == "__main__":
    launch_yono_chatbot()

"""
Application package for the YONO Business RAG Chatbot.

"""

FILE: src\dlh_ai\genai\application\ui.py

"""
YONO Business RAG Chatbot - Pure-Python HTTP Frontend UI.

This module serves a self-contained HTML/CSS/JS chat interface using only
the Python standard library (http.server). It is launched by
``Launcher.py`` as a separate process on the port supplied through the
``CDSW_APP_PORT`` environment variable (default: 8100).

All chat traffic is proxied from the browser to the FastAPI backend via a
``/api/chat`` POST handler on this server, so the browser never needs
direct access to the backend port. The page has a sidebar with per-session
chat history (stored client-side in ``localStorage``, no backend session
store required) and a "New chat" action.

Run standalone (development):
    python src/dlh_ai/genai/application/ui.py

Environment variables
---------------------
CB_BACKEND_URL : str
    Base URL of the FastAPI backend. Example: ``http://127.0.0.1:8008``.
CDSW_APP_PORT : str
    TCP port this server should listen on. Defaults to ``8100``.
"""

import json
import logging
import os
import time
import urllib.request
import urllib.error
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer

LOGGER = logging.getLogger("dlh_ai")

_BACKEND_URL: str = os.environ.get("CB_BACKEND_URL", "http://127.0.0.1:8008")
_CHAT_ENDPOINT: str = f"{_BACKEND_URL}/api/v1/chat"
_HEALTH_ENDPOINT: str = f"{_BACKEND_URL}/health"
_UI_PORT: int = int(os.environ.get("CDSW_APP_PORT", "8100"))

<script>
  const chat        = document.getElementById('chat');
  const queryEl      = document.getElementById('query');
  const sendBtn      = document.getElementById('send-btn');
  const topkEl       = document.getElementById('topk');
  const topkVal      = document.getElementById('topk-val');
  const statusEl     = document.getElementById('status');
  const sidebarStatusEl = document.getElementById('sidebar-status');
  const sidebarStatusTxt = sidebarStatusEl.querySelector('.txt');
  const retryBtn     = document.getElementById('retry-btn');
  const sidebar      = document.getElementById('sidebar');
  const sidebarOverlay = document.getElementById('sidebar-overlay');
  const menuBtn      = document.getElementById('menu-btn');
  const sidebarCloseBtn = document.getElementById('sidebar-close');
  const newChatBtn   = document.getElementById('new-chat-btn');
  const sessionListEl = document.getElementById('session-list');

  const STORAGE_KEY = 'yono_chat_sessions';
  const ACTIVE_KEY  = 'yono_active_session';
  const CHAT_TIMEOUT_MS = 95000;
  const HEALTH_POLL_MS  = 20000;

  // crypto.randomUUID() only exists in secure contexts (https, or
  // http://localhost). This app is often served over plain http on an
  // internal host/IP, where crypto.randomUUID is undefined and calling it
  // throws - which would silently stop this whole script (including the
  // send-button click handler) from ever being registered. Fall back to a
  // manual UUID v4 generator so the page works on any origin.
  function genId() {{
    if (window.crypto && typeof crypto.randomUUID === 'function') {{
      try {{ return crypto.randomUUID(); }} catch (e) {{ /* fall through */ }}
    }}
    return 'xxxxxxxx-xxxx-4xxx-yxxx-xxxxxxxxxxxx'.replace(/[xy]/g, (c) => {{
      const r = Math.random() * 16 | 0;
      const v = c === 'x' ? r : (r & 0x3) | 0x8;
      return v.toString(16);
    }});
  }}

  // Session storage (client-side chat history, no backend session store)
  function loadSessions() {{
    try {{
      const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
      return raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : [];
    }} catch (e) {{
      return [];
    }}
  }}

  function saveSessions(sessions) {{
    try {{
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(sessions));
    }} catch (e) {{ /* storage full or disabled, ignore */ }}
  }}

  function makeSession() {{
    return {{ id: 'sess-' + genId(), title: 'New chat', createdAt: Date.now(), messages: [] }};
  }}

  let sessions = loadSessions();
  let activeId = localStorage.getItem(ACTIVE_KEY);
  if (!sessions.length || !sessions.some(s => s.id === activeId)) {{
    const s = makeSession();
    sessions = [s, ...sessions];
    activeId = s.id;
  }}

  function getActive() {{
    return sessions.find(s => s.id === activeId) || sessions[0];
  }}

  function persist() {{
    saveSessions(sessions);
    localStorage.setItem(ACTIVE_KEY, activeId);
  }}

  function formatTime(ts) {{
    return new Date(ts).toLocaleTimeString([], {{ hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }});
  }}

  function renderSidebar() {{
    sessionListEl.innerHTML = '';
    sessions.forEach(s => {{
      const item = document.createElement('div');
      item.className = 'session-item' + (s.id === activeId ? ' active' : '');
      item.innerHTML =
        '<span class="title">' + escHtml(s.title) + '</span>' +
        '<span class="time">' + formatTime(s.createdAt) + '</span>' +
        '<button class="delete-btn" title="Delete chat">&times;</button>';
      item.addEventListener('click', (e) => {{
        if (e.target.classList.contains('delete-btn')) return;
        switchSession(s.id);
      }});
      item.querySelector('.delete-btn').addEventListener('click', (e) => {{
        e.stopPropagation();
        deleteSession(s.id);
      }});
      sessionListEl.appendChild(item);
    }});
  }}

  function switchSession(id) {{
    if (id === activeId) {{ closeSidebarOnMobile(); return; }}
    activeId = id;
    persist();
    renderChatFromSession();
    renderSidebar();
    closeSidebarOnMobile();
  }}

  function deleteSession(id) {{
    sessions = sessions.filter(s => s.id !== id);
    if (!sessions.length) sessions = [makeSession()];
    if (id === activeId) activeId = sessions[0].id;
    persist();
    renderChatFromSession();
    renderSidebar();
  }}

  function startNewChat() {{
    const s = makeSession();
    sessions = [s, ...sessions];
    activeId = s.id;
    persist();
    renderChatFromSession();
    renderSidebar();
    closeSidebarOnMobile();
    queryEl.focus();
  }}

  function renderChatFromSession() {{
    chat.innerHTML = '';
    const session = getActive();
    if (!session.messages.length) {{
      appendBot(
        'Welcome to YONO Business Assistant - State Bank of India\\'s AI-powered banking helpdesk.\\n\\n' +
        'I can help you with ATM & Debit Card services, fund transfers, account enquiries, ' +
        'Fixed Deposits, loan details, and all other YONO features.\\n\\n' +
        'Select a suggestion below or type your question.',
        null,
        false
      );
      return;
    }}
    session.messages.forEach(m => {{
      if (m.role === 'user') appendUser(m.text, false);
      else if (m.role === 'error') appendError(m.text, false);
      else appendBot(m.text, m.meta || null, false);
    }});
  }}

  function recordMessage(role, text, meta) {{
    const session = getActive();
    session.messages.push({{ role, text, meta: meta || null }});
    if (role === 'user' && session.title === 'New chat') {{
      session.title = text.length > 40 ? text.slice(0, 40) + '...' : text;
    }}
    persist();
    renderSidebar();
  }}

  // Sidebar open/close (mobile overlay)
  function openSidebar() {{
    sidebar.classList.add('open');
    sidebarOverlay.classList.add('open');
  }}
  function closeSidebar() {{
    sidebar.classList.remove('open');
    sidebarOverlay.classList.remove('open');
  }}
  function closeSidebarOnMobile() {{
    if (window.innerWidth < 769) closeSidebar();
  }}
  menuBtn.addEventListener('click', openSidebar);
  sidebarCloseBtn.addEventListener('click', closeSidebar);
  sidebarOverlay.addEventListener('click', closeSidebar);
  newChatBtn.addEventListener('click', startNewChat);

  // Sync top_k display label with slider.
  topkEl.addEventListener('input', () => {{ topkVal.textContent = topkEl.value; }});

  // Auto-resize textarea as user types.
  queryEl.addEventListener('input', () => {{
    queryEl.style.height = 'auto';
    queryEl.style.height = Math.min(queryEl.scrollHeight, 120) + 'px';
  }});

  // Send on Enter (Shift+Enter = newline).
  queryEl.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {{
    if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey) {{ e.preventDefault(); sendMessage(); }}
  }});
  sendBtn.addEventListener('click', sendMessage);

  // Chip click copies text into input.
  document.querySelectorAll('.chip').forEach(btn => {{
    btn.addEventListener('click', () => {{
      queryEl.value = btn.textContent;
      queryEl.dispatchEvent(new Event('input'));
      queryEl.focus();
    }});
  }});

  // Helpers
  function setStatus(text, cls) {{
    statusEl.textContent = text;
    statusEl.className = cls;
    sidebarStatusTxt.textContent = text;
    sidebarStatusEl.className = cls;
  }}

  function scrollBottom() {{
    chat.scrollTop = chat.scrollHeight;
  }}

  function escHtml(s) {{
    return String(s).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;')
            .replace(/'/g,'&#39;').replace(/`/g,'&#96;');
  }}

  /* Renders a safe subset of Markdown (bold, bullet/numbered lists) plus a
     few common inline LaTeX delimiters the LLM emits (e.g. $\\rightarrow$).
     Input is HTML-escaped first, so only tags this function itself adds
     ever reach innerHTML - raw text from the model can never inject markup. */
  function mdToHtml(raw) {{
    let text = escHtml(raw);

    const LATEX_SYMBOLS = {{
      rightarrow: '&rarr;', leftarrow: '&larr;', Rightarrow: '&rArr;',
      times: '&times;', approx: '&asymp;', leq: '&le;', geq: '&ge;'
    }};
    text = text
      .replace(/\$\\\\?([a-zA-Z]+)\$/g, (m, name) => LATEX_SYMBOLS[name] || m)
      .replace(/\$([^$\\n]+)\$/g, '$1');

    text = text.replace(/\*\*([^*\\n]+)\*\*/g, '<strong>$1</strong>');

    const lines = text.split('\\n');
    const htmlParts = [];
    let listItems = [];
    let listTag = null;
    let paraLines = [];

    function flushList() {{
      if (listItems.length) {{
        const tag = listTag || 'ul';
        htmlParts.push('<' + tag + '>' + listItems.map((i) => '<li>' + i + '</li>').join('') + '</' + tag + '>');
        listItems = [];
        listTag = null;
      }}
    }}

    function flushPara() {{
      if (paraLines.length) {{
        htmlParts.push('<p>' + paraLines.join('<br>') + '</p>');
        paraLines = [];
      }}
    }}

    lines.forEach((line) => {{
      const bulletMatch = line.match(/^\s*[*-]\s+(.*)/);
      const numberMatch = line.match(/^\s*\d+[.)]\s+(.*)/);
      if (bulletMatch) {{
        flushPara();
        if (listTag && listTag !== 'ul') flushList();
        listTag = 'ul';
        listItems.push(bulletMatch[1]);
      }} else if (numberMatch) {{
        flushPara();
        if (listTag && listTag !== 'ol') flushList();
        listTag = 'ol';
        listItems.push(numberMatch[1]);
      }} else if (line.trim() === '') {{
        flushList();
        flushPara();
      }} else {{
        flushList();
        paraLines.push(line);
      }}
    }});
    flushList();
    flushPara();

    return htmlParts.join('') || '<p></p>';
  }}

  function appendUser(text, record) {{
    const div = document.createElement('div');
    div.className = 'msg user';
    div.innerHTML = '<div class="bubble">' + escHtml(text) + '</div>';
    chat.appendChild(div);
    scrollBottom();
    if (record !== false) recordMessage('user', text, null);
    return div;
  }}

  function appendTyping() {{
    const div = document.createElement('div');
    div.className = 'msg bot';
    div.innerHTML = '<div class="bubble typing"><span></span><span></span><span></span></div>';
    chat.appendChild(div);
    scrollBottom();
    return div;
  }}

  function appendBot(text, meta, record) {{
    const div = document.createElement('div');
    div.className = 'msg bot';
    let html = '<div class="bubble">' + mdToHtml(text);
    if (meta) {{
      html += '<div class="meta">'
            + '<span>Category: ' + escHtml(meta.category) + '</span>'
            + '<span>Type: ' + escHtml(meta.doc_type) + '</span>'
            + '<span>Confidence: ' + meta.pct + '%</span>'
            + '</div>';
      if (meta.sources && meta.sources.length > 1) {{
        html += '<div class="sources"><strong>Supporting sources:</strong>';
        meta.sources.forEach((s, i) => {{
          const sc = Math.round((s.score || 0) * 100);
          html += '<div>[' + (i + 1) + '] ' + escHtml(s.category || '-')
               + ' - ' + escHtml((s.question || '-').substring(0, 80))
               + '  (Sno: ' + escHtml(String(s.sno)) + ', score: ' + sc + '%)</div>';
        }});
        html += '</div>';
      }}
      if (meta.source_table_link) {{
        html += '<div class="sources"><a href="' + escHtml(meta.source_table_link)
             + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">View source row in table &rarr;</a></div>';
      }}
    }}
    html += '</div>';
    div.innerHTML = html;
    chat.appendChild(div);
    scrollBottom();
    if (record !== false) recordMessage('bot', text, meta || null);
    return div;
  }}

  function appendError(msg, record) {{
    const div = document.createElement('div');
    div.className = 'msg bot';
    div.innerHTML = '<div class="bubble" style="border-color:#b91c1c;color:#f87171;">Error: ' + escHtml(msg) + '</div>';
    chat.appendChild(div);
    scrollBottom();
    if (record !== false) recordMessage('error', msg, null);
  }}

  async function sendMessage() {{
    const text = queryEl.value.trim();
    if (!text || sendBtn.disabled) return;

    queryEl.value = '';
    queryEl.style.height = 'auto';
    sendBtn.disabled = true;
    setStatus('Thinking...', 'thinking');

    appendUser(text, true);
    const typingDiv = appendTyping();

    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), CHAT_TIMEOUT_MS);

    try {{
      const res = await fetch('/api/chat', {{
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {{ 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }},
        signal: controller.signal,
        body: JSON.stringify({{
          query: text,
          top_k: parseInt(topkEl.value, 10),
          session_id: activeId,
        }}),
      }});

      let data;
      try {{
        data = await res.json();
      }} catch (parseErr) {{
        throw new Error('Backend returned an invalid response.');
      }}

      typingDiv.remove();

      if (!res.ok || data.error) {{
        appendError(data.message || data.detail || ('Request failed (HTTP ' + res.status + ').'), true);
        setStatus('Error', 'offline');
      }} else {{
        const pct = Math.round((data.score || 0) * 100);
        appendBot(data.answer || 'No answer returned.', {{
          category: data.category || '-',
          doc_type:  data.doc_type  || '-',
          pct,
          sources: data.sources || [],
          source_table_link: data.source_table_link || null,
        }}, true);
        setStatus('Connected', 'online');
      }}
    }} catch (err) {{
      typingDiv.remove();
      if (err.name === 'AbortError') {{
        appendError('The backend took too long to respond. Please try again.', true);
      }} else {{
        appendError('Network error: ' + err.message, true);
      }}
      setStatus('Offline', 'offline');
    }} finally {{
      clearTimeout(timer);
      sendBtn.disabled = false;
      queryEl.focus();
    }}
  }}

  function checkBackend() {{
    return fetch('/api/health')
      .then(r => {{
        const ok = r.ok;
        setStatus(ok ? 'Connected' : 'Backend Offline', ok ? 'online' : 'offline');
        return ok;
      }})
      .catch(() => {{
        setStatus('Backend Offline', 'offline');
        return false;
      }});
  }}

  retryBtn.addEventListener('click', checkBackend);

  // Initial render
  renderSidebar();
  renderChatFromSession();
  checkBackend();
  setInterval(checkBackend, HEALTH_POLL_MS);
</script>
</body>
</html>
"""


def _build_html() -> str:
    """Render the HTML page, injecting suggestion chips.

    Returns
    -------
    str
        Complete HTML document as a string.
    """
    chips_html = "".join(
        f'<button class="chip">{label}</button>'
        for label in _SUGGESTIONS
    )
    return _HTML_TEMPLATE.format(chips=chips_html)


# Pre-render once at import time, the HTML never changes at runtime.
_PAGE: bytes = _build_html().encode("utf-8")


def _check_backend() -> bool:
    """Ping the backend health endpoint and return True if healthy."""
    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(_HEALTH_ENDPOINT, timeout=5) as r:
            return r.status == 200
    except Exception as exc:
        LOGGER.warning("Backend health check failed: %s: %s", type(exc).__name__, exc)
        return False


def _proxy_chat(body: bytes) -> tuple[int, bytes]:
    """Forward a chat request to the FastAPI backend and return (status, body).

    Parameters
    ----------
    body : bytes
        Raw JSON request body from the browser.

    Returns
    -------
    tuple[int, bytes]
        HTTP status code and response body bytes.
    """
    req = urllib.request.Request(
        _CHAT_ENDPOINT,
        data=body,
        headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"},
        method="POST",
    )
    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=90) as resp:
            return resp.status, resp.read()
    except urllib.error.HTTPError as exc:
        return exc.code, exc.read()
    except Exception as exc:
        LOGGER.error("Chat proxy request failed: %s: %s", type(exc).__name__, exc)
        payload = json.dumps({"error": True, "message": f"Network error: {exc}"}).encode()
        return 502, payload


class _Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    """Minimal HTTP request handler.

    Serves the HTML page at ``GET /`` and proxies chat/health API calls.
    """

    def log_message(self, format: str, *args: object) -> None:  # noqa: A002
        """Suppress default access log to keep the console clean."""

    def do_GET(self) -> None:
        """Handle GET requests."""
        if self.path in ("/", "/index.html"):
            self._send(200, "text/html; charset=utf-8", _PAGE)
        elif self.path == "/api/health":
            ok = _check_backend()
            payload = json.dumps({"status": "ok" if ok else "offline"}).encode()
            self._send(200, "application/json", payload)
        else:
            self._send(404, "text/plain", b"Not found")

    def do_POST(self) -> None:
        """Handle POST requests, proxy /api/chat to the FastAPI backend."""
        if self.path == "/api/chat":
            length = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length", 0))
            body = self.rfile.read(length) if length > 0 else b"{}"
            status, response_body = _proxy_chat(body)
            self._send(status, "application/json", response_body)
        else:
            self._send(404, "text/plain", b"Not found")

    def _send(self, status: int, content_type: str, body: bytes) -> None:
        """Write status line, headers, and body to the socket."""
        self.send_response(status)
        self.send_header("Content-Type", content_type)
        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
        self.end_headers()
        self.wfile.write(body)


def main() -> None:
    """Start the HTTP server and block until interrupted."""
    LOGGER.info("YONO Business UI starting on port %d...", _UI_PORT)
    LOGGER.info("Backend target: %s", _BACKEND_URL)

    for attempt in range(1, 4):
        if _check_backend():
            LOGGER.info("Backend health-check: OK")
            break
        LOGGER.info("Waiting for backend... (attempt %d/3)", attempt)
        time.sleep(3)
    else:
        LOGGER.warning("Backend not responding, UI will start anyway.")

    # ThreadingHTTPServer handles each request on its own thread, so a slow
    # chat request (waiting on the LLM) can never block health checks or
    # other concurrent requests from being served.
    print("CDSW_APP_PORT =", os.getenv("CDSW_APP_PORT"))
    print("CDSW_READONLY_PORT = ", os.getenv("CDSW_READONLY_PORT"))
    print("CB_APP_PORT =", os.getenv("CB_APP_PORT"))
    print("UI_PORT =", _UI_PORT)
    print("CDSW_PUBLIC_PORT =", os.getenv("CDSW_PUBLIC_PORT"))

    server = ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", _UI_PORT), _Handler)
    url = f"http://127.0.0.1:{_UI_PORT}"
    print(f"  Yono Business ChatBot UI  →  {url}")
    print(f"  Backend proxy      →  {_BACKEND_URL}")
    print("  Press Ctrl+C to stop.\n")
    LOGGER.info("UI ready at http://127.0.0.1%d", _UI_PORT)
    LOGGER.info("Press Ctrl+C to stop.")
    try:
        server.serve_forever()
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        pass
    finally:
        server.server_close()

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

"""
Backend application package for the YONO Business RAG Chatbot.

Provides the :obj:`app` FastAPI application instance and the
administration ingestion endpoint.

Usage
-----
Start with uvicorn::

    uvicorn dlh_ai.genai.application.backend.main:app --reload --port 8000

Or with gunicorn for production::

    gunicorn dlh_ai.genai.application.backend.main:app \\
        -k uvicorn.workers.UvicornWorker \\
        --workers 4 \\
        --bind 0.0.0.0:8000
"""

from .main import app

__all__ = ["app"]



####################################################################################################
FILE: src\dlh_ai\genai\application\backend\main.py
####################################################################################################

"""
YONO Business RAG Chatbot - FastAPI Backend Application.

This module is the application entry point. It wires together:
* :func:`build_es_client`: Elasticsearch client with large connection pool.
* :class:`EmbeddingService`: GPU-accelerated SentenceTransformer with FP16
  and dynamic micro-batching.
* :class:`LlmService`: DLH vLLM client with persistent connection pool
  and exponential-backoff retries.
* :class:`RetrievalService`: kNN / hybrid vector search.
* :class:`ChatHistoryService`: optional Elasticsearch-backed multi-turn chat
  history and server-side session-ID generation.
* :class:`PromptManager`: Jinja2 prompt templates.

Performance design (thousands of concurrent users):
* A **dedicated ``ThreadPoolExecutor``** (size = ``RAG__THREAD_POOL_SIZE``)
  offloads every blocking call (embedding + Elasticsearch + LLM) so the
  asyncio event loop is never stalled.
* **``asyncio.get_running_loop()``** is used instead of the deprecated
  ``get_event_loop()`` for Python 3.10+ correctness.
* The embedding service uses a CUDA GPU (FP16) and a micro-batch worker
  that coalesces concurrent single-query encodes into large GPU batches.
* The LLM service reuses a keep-alive HTTP connection pool, no TCP
  handshake per request.
* ``/metrics`` is exposed via ``prometheus_fastapi_instrumentator`` for
  Prometheus scraping.

Run (development):
    uvicorn dlh_ai.genai.application.backend.main:app --reload

Run (production, tune workers to 2x CPU cores):
    gunicorn dlh_ai.genai.application.backend.main:app \\
        -k uvicorn.workers.UvicornWorker \\
        --workers 8 \\
        --worker-connections 1000 \\
        --bind 0.0.0.0:8000
"""

import asyncio
import logging
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
from pathlib import Path
from typing import AsyncIterator

from elasticsearch import Elasticsearch
from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException, Request, status
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
from fastapi.middleware.gzip import GZipMiddleware
from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse
from fastapi.staticfiles import StaticFiles

from dlh_ai.config import get_settings
from dlh_ai.genai.rag import (
    ChatHistoryService,
    ChatRequest,
    ChatResponse,
    ClarificationNeededError,
    EmbeddingService,
    GuardrailService,
    GuardrailViolationError,
    HallucinationDetectedError,
    IngestionResult,
    InvalidCitationError,
    LlmService,
    OutOfDomainError,
    PIIDetectedError,
    ProfanityDetectedError,
    PromptInjectionDetectedError,
    RerankerService,
    RetrievalService,
    ToxicContentError,
    UnsupportedLanguageError,
    YonoRagError,
    build_es_client,
    create_or_update_index,
    ingest_knowledge_base,
)
from dlh_ai.genai.prompt import PromptManager

LOGGER = logging.getLogger("dlh_ai")

# Application-level singletons, shared across all requests --------------------

_embedding_service: EmbeddingService | None = None
_llm_service: LlmService | None = None
_reranker_service: RerankerService | None = None
_chat_history_service: ChatHistoryService | None = None
_retrieval_service: RetrievalService | None = None

# Dedicated thread pool for all blocking I/O (embedding + ES + LLM).
# Sized via config; default 32 is enough for ~1000 concurrent users given
# typical LLM latencies of 1-3s. Increase for higher QPS.
_thread_pool: ThreadPoolExecutor | None = None

# Prompt manager - construction only reads YAML files from disk (no GPU/
# network dependencies), so it's safe to build once at import time. Shared
# by the RetrievalService's prompt rendering (via _lifespan below) and by
# the guardrail-scenario error handler further down this module.
_prompt_manager = PromptManager()

# Guardrail-scenario -> prompt key mapping (see prompt/guardrail_messages.yaml).
# Order matters: this is checked top-to-bottom with isinstance(), so more
# specific exception subclasses must be listed before their common base
# (GuardrailViolationError) to get their own message instead of "generic".
_GUARDRAIL_PROMPT_AGENT = "guardrail_messages"
_GUARDRAIL_MESSAGE_KEYS: tuple[tuple[type[YonoRagError], str], ...] = (
    (ClarificationNeededError, "clarification_needed"),
    (UnsupportedLanguageError, "unsupported_language"),
    (ProfanityDetectedError, "profanity"),
    (ToxicContentError, "toxicity"),
    (PIIDetectedError, "pii_detected"),
    (PromptInjectionDetectedError, "prompt_injection"),
    (OutOfDomainError, "out_of_domain"),
    (HallucinationDetectedError, "hallucination"),
    (InvalidCitationError, "invalid_citation"),
    (GuardrailViolationError, "generic"),
)


def _guardrail_message_key(exc: YonoRagError) -> str:
    """Map a domain exception instance to its scenario-specific prompt key.

    Falls back to ``"service_unavailable"`` for any :class:`YonoRagError`
    that is not a guardrail-related scenario (embedding, Elasticsearch,
    LLM, ingestion, configuration errors, etc.).
    """
    for exc_type, key in _GUARDRAIL_MESSAGE_KEYS:
        if isinstance(exc, exc_type):
            return key
    return "service_unavailable"


# Lifespan: initialise heavy resources once at startup -------------------------


@asynccontextmanager
async def _lifespan(app: FastAPI) -> AsyncIterator[None]:
    """Initialise application-level singletons on startup and clean up on shutdown.

    Parameters
    ----------
    app : FastAPI
        The FastAPI application instance (unused but required by the protocol).

    Yields
    ------
    None
        Control is yielded to the running application after setup completes.

    Raises
    ------
    YonoRagError
        Raised during startup if the Elasticsearch client, embedding model,
        LLM client, or index creation fails.
    """
    global _embedding_service, _llm_service, _reranker_service, _chat_history_service, _retrieval_service, _thread_pool  # noqa: PLW0603

    LOGGER.info("YONO Business RAG backend starting up...")
    settings = get_settings()

    # Thread pool -----------------------------------------------------------
    pool_size: int = settings.rag.thread_pool_size
    _thread_pool = ThreadPoolExecutor(
        max_workers=pool_size,
        thread_name_prefix="rag-worker",
    )
    LOGGER.info("Thread pool initialised, max_workers=%d.", pool_size)

    # Elasticsearch client ----------------------------------------------------
    LOGGER.info("Initialising Elasticsearch client...")
    es_client = build_es_client(
        host=settings.rag.elasticsearch_host,
        api_key=settings.rag.elasticsearch_api_key,
        request_timeout=settings.rag.request_timeout,
        max_retries=settings.rag.max_retries,
        verify_certs=settings.rag.verify_certs,
        connections_per_node=settings.rag.connections_per_node,
    )

    # Embedding model (GPU) ----------------------------------------------------
    LOGGER.info("Loading GPU-accelerated embedding model from HDFS...")
    _embedding_service = EmbeddingService(
        hdfs_model_path=settings.rag.hdfs_model_path,
        expected_dimension=settings.rag.embedding_dimension,
        use_fp16=settings.rag.embedding_fp16,
        gpu_batch_size=settings.rag.embedding_gpu_batch_size,
        dynamic_batching=settings.rag.embedding_dynamic_batching,
        dynamic_batch_max_size=settings.rag.embedding_dynamic_batch_max_size,
    )

    # LLM client -----------------------------------------------------------
    LOGGER.info(
        "Initialising DLH LLM client. Model: '%s' | Base URL: '%s'",
        settings.llm.model_name,
        settings.llm.base_url,
    )
    _llm_service = LlmService(
        model_name=settings.llm.model_name,
        base_url=settings.llm.base_url,
        api_key=settings.llm.api_key,
        verify_ssl=settings.llm.verify_ssl,
        temperature=settings.llm.temperature,
        max_tokens=settings.llm.max_tokens,
        request_timeout=settings.llm.request_timeout,
        connection_pool_size=settings.llm.connection_pool_size,
        max_retries=settings.llm.max_retries,
        use_max_completion_tokens=settings.llm.use_max_completion_tokens,
    )
    _llm_service.load()

    # Guardrails (heuristic PII/profanity/toxicity/topic checks) --------------
    LOGGER.info(
        "Initialising guardrail service. PII redaction: %s | Profanity "
        "filter: %s | Toxicity filter: %s",
        settings.guardrails.enable_pii_redaction,
        settings.guardrails.enable_profanity_filter,
        settings.guardrails.enable_toxicity_filter,
    )
    guardrail_service = GuardrailService(
        pii_entities=settings.guardrails.pii_entities,
        enable_pii_redaction=settings.guardrails.enable_pii_redaction,
        enable_profanity_filter=settings.guardrails.enable_profanity_filter,
        enable_toxicity_filter=settings.guardrails.enable_toxicity_filter,
        toxicity_threshold=settings.guardrails.toxicity_threshold,
        grounding_threshold=settings.guardrails.grounding_threshold,
        detoxify_checkpoint_path=settings.guardrails.detoxify_checkpoint_path,
    )

    # Reranker (optional ONNX/torch cross-encoder second-stage reranking) ----
    LOGGER.info(
        "Initialising reranker service. Enabled: %s | ONNX: %s",
        settings.reranker.enabled,
        settings.reranker.use_onnx,
    )
    _reranker_service = RerankerService(
        enabled=settings.reranker.enabled,
        model_path=settings.reranker.model_path,
        use_onnx=settings.reranker.use_onnx,
        candidate_multiplier=settings.reranker.candidate_multiplier,
        max_length=settings.reranker.max_length,
        batch_size=settings.reranker.batch_size,
    )

    # Chat history (optional Elasticsearch-backed multi-turn memory) ---------
    LOGGER.info(
        "Initialising chat history service. Enabled: %s",
        settings.chat_history.enabled,
    )
    chat_history_client: Elasticsearch | None = None
    if settings.chat_history.enabled:
        chat_history_client = build_es_client(
            host=settings.chat_history.elasticsearch_host,
            api_key=settings.chat_history.elasticsearch_api_key,
            request_timeout=settings.rag.request_timeout,
            max_retries=settings.rag.max_retries,
            verify_certs=settings.rag.verify_certs,
            connections_per_node=settings.rag.connections_per_node,
        )
    _chat_history_service = ChatHistoryService(
        enabled=settings.chat_history.enabled,
        client=chat_history_client,
        index_name=settings.chat_history.index_name,
        max_history_turns=settings.chat_history.max_history_turns,
    )

    # Retrieval service -------------------------------------------------------
    _retrieval_service = RetrievalService(
        client=es_client,
        embedding_service=_embedding_service,
        llm_service=_llm_service,
        index_name=settings.rag.index_name,
        prompt_manager=_prompt_manager,
        guardrail_service=guardrail_service,
        reranker_service=_reranker_service,
        chat_history_service=_chat_history_service,
        source_table_url_template=settings.rag.source_table_url_template,
        enable_source_table_link=settings.rag.enable_source_table_link,
    )

    LOGGER.info("YONO Business RAG backend ready.")
    yield

    # Graceful shutdown -------------------------------------------------------
    LOGGER.info("YONO Business RAG backend shutting down...")

    if _embedding_service is not None:
        _embedding_service.shutdown()
        LOGGER.info("Embedding service batch worker stopped.")

    if _llm_service is not None:
        _llm_service.shutdown()
        LOGGER.info("LLM HTTP connection pool closed.")

    if _thread_pool is not None:
        # cancel_futures=True (Python 3.9+): pending work items that have not
        # yet started are cancelled immediately instead of being waited upon.
        _thread_pool.shutdown(wait=True, cancel_futures=True)
        LOGGER.info("Thread pool shut down.")

    LOGGER.info("Shutdown complete.")


# FastAPI application -----------------------------------------------------------

app = FastAPI(
    title="YONO Business RAG Chatbot API",
    description=(
        "Enterprise-grade Retrieval-Augmented Generation chatbot for the "
        "YONO Business digital banking platform. GPU-accelerated embeddings, "
        "persistent LLM connection pool, dynamic micro-batching."
    ),
    version="2.0.0",
    lifespan=_lifespan,
)

# Compress responses > 1 KB, cuts bandwidth by 60-80% for JSON payloads.
app.add_middleware(GZipMiddleware, minimum_size=1024)

# Allow the frontend SPA to call this API from a browser.
#
# allow_origins=["*"] combined with allow_credentials=True is an invalid/
# unsafe combination per the Fetch/CORS spec (browsers reject wildcard
# origins on credentialed requests, and permitting it would allow any site
# to make credentialed calls). Origins must be configured explicitly via
# API__CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS. When no origin is configured, credentials are
# disabled so the middleware still starts safely in a fresh environment.
_cors_settings = get_settings()
_cors_origins: list[str] = _cors_settings.api.cors_allowed_origins
app.add_middleware(
    CORSMiddleware,
    allow_origins=_cors_origins,
    allow_credentials=bool(_cors_origins),
    allow_methods=["GET", "POST", "OPTIONS"],
    allow_headers=["*"],
)


# Global exception handler -------------------------------------------------------


@app.exception_handler(YonoRagError)
async def _rag_error_handler(request: Request, exc: YonoRagError) -> JSONResponse:
    """Handle all domain-specific RAG errors with a scenario-specific JSON body.

    Every :class:`YonoRagError` subclass maps to its own user-facing
    ``message`` (see ``prompt/guardrail_messages.yaml``) so a customer
    blocked for, say, an out-of-domain question sees actionable guidance
    instead of the same generic "temporarily unavailable" text shown for a
    genuine backend outage. The HTTP status code stays ``503`` for every
    case (matches the documented API contract); only ``error`` and
    ``message`` vary by scenario.
    """
    LOGGER.error(
        "RAG pipeline error on %s %s: %s",
        request.method,
        request.url.path,
        exc,
    )
    message_key = _guardrail_message_key(exc)
    message = _prompt_manager.get_prompt(_GUARDRAIL_PROMPT_AGENT, message_key).strip()
    error_code = (
        "service_unavailable"
        if message_key == "service_unavailable"
        else f"guardrail_{message_key}"
    )
    return JSONResponse(
        status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
        content={"error": error_code, "message": message},
    )


# Health check ---------------------------------------------------------------


@app.get(
    "/health",
    summary="Health check",
    description="Returns 200 if the application is healthy and all services are ready.",
    tags=["Operations"],
)
async def health_check() -> JSONResponse:
    """Return the actual readiness status of the backend.

    Checks that the embedding model, LLM client, and retrieval service
    (which in turn owns the Elasticsearch client) were all successfully
    initialised during startup. Returns 503 instead of a blanket "ok"
    when any dependency failed to come up, so orchestrators (Kubernetes
    liveness/readiness probes, load balancers) can correctly detect and
    route around an unhealthy instance.

    Returns
    -------
    JSONResponse
        200 with ``{"status": "ok", ...}`` when all services are ready,
        or 503 with ``{"status": "unavailable", ...}`` and the list of
        services that are not yet initialised.

    Examples
    --------
    GET /health returns {"status": "ok", "service": "yono-rag-chatbot"}
    """
    not_ready: list[str] = []
    if _embedding_service is None:
        not_ready.append("embedding_service")
    if _llm_service is None:
        not_ready.append("llm_service")
    if _retrieval_service is None:
        not_ready.append("retrieval_service")

    if not_ready:
        return JSONResponse(
            status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
            content={
                "status": "unavailable",
                "service": "yono-rag-chatbot",
                "not_ready": not_ready,
            },
        )

    return JSONResponse(
        status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK,
        content={"status": "ok", "service": "yono-rag-chatbot"},
    )


# Chat endpoint ---------------------------------------------------------------


@app.post(
    "/api/v1/chat",
    response_model=ChatResponse,
    summary="YONO Business RAG chat",
    description=(
        "Submit a natural-language banking question and receive the top "
        "knowledge-base answer along with supporting source documents."
    ),
    tags=["Chat"],
    status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK,
)
async def chat(request: ChatRequest) -> ChatResponse:
    """Handle a user chat message and return a RAG-powered answer.

    The blocking RAG pipeline (embedding → Elasticsearch → LLM) is
    offloaded to the dedicated ``_thread_pool`` so the asyncio event loop
    is never blocked, enabling thousands of concurrent in-flight requests.

    Parameters
    ----------
    request : ChatRequest
        Validated request body containing the query and optional settings.

    Returns
    -------
    ChatResponse
        Best-match answer, category, confidence score, and supporting sources.

    Raises
    ------
    HTTPException
        HTTP 503 when the retrieval service is not yet initialised.
    YonoRagError
        Propagated from the RAG pipeline; converted to HTTP 503 by the
        global exception handler.
    """
    if _retrieval_service is None:
        LOGGER.error("Retrieval service not initialised. Chat request rejected.")
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
            detail="The chatbot service is not yet ready. Please retry in a moment.",
        )

    LOGGER.info(
        "Chat request received, session=%s query='%s'",
        request.session_id,
        request.query[:80],
    )

    # Offload the entire blocking pipeline to the dedicated thread pool.
    # asyncio.get_running_loop() is the correct API for Python 3.10+.
    loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
    response: ChatResponse = await loop.run_in_executor(
        _thread_pool,
        _retrieval_service.answer_query,
        request,
    )

    return response


# Ingestion endpoint (admin / offline use) -------------------------------------


@app.post(
    "/api/v1/admin/ingest",
    response_model=IngestionResult,
    summary="Re-ingest knowledge base",
    description=(
        "Triggers a knowledge-base re-ingestion from the configured CSV "
        "file. By default this appends/updates documents in the existing "
        "Elasticsearch index; pass 'recreate=true' to instead delete and "
        "recreate the index (all existing documents are lost). "
        "SECURITY: this endpoint has no authentication of its own, it "
        "MUST be restricted to admin operators at the network/gateway "
        "layer (e.g. internal-only ingress, VPN, or a reverse-proxy "
        "auth rule) before this service is exposed. See deployment docs."
    ),
    tags=["Admin"],
    status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK,
)
async def ingest_knowledge_base_endpoint(recreate: bool = False) -> IngestionResult:
    """Re-ingest the YONO FAQ knowledge base into Elasticsearch.

    Parameters
    ----------
    recreate : bool, optional
        If ``True``, delete and recreate the Elasticsearch index before
        ingesting, all existing documents are lost. Default is ``False``
        (the destructive path must be opted into explicitly per request).

    Returns
    -------
    IngestionResult
        Summary of total rows, successful, and failed document counts.

    Raises
    ------
    HTTPException
        HTTP 503 when the embedding or ES service is not ready.
    YonoRagError
        Propagated from the ingestion pipeline on any failure.
    """
    if _embedding_service is None or _retrieval_service is None:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
            detail="Services not initialised. Cannot run ingestion.",
        )

    emb_svc: EmbeddingService = _embedding_service
    ret_svc: RetrievalService = _retrieval_service

    settings = get_settings()
    loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()

    # Step 1: create the index if missing, or recreate it if explicitly requested.
    await loop.run_in_executor(
        _thread_pool,
        lambda: create_or_update_index(
            client=ret_svc.client,
            index_name=settings.rag.index_name,
            embedding_dimension=settings.rag.embedding_dimension,
            recreate=recreate,
        ),
    )

    # Step 2: ingest CSV (blocking), GPU-batched via EmbeddingService.
    result: IngestionResult = await loop.run_in_executor(
        _thread_pool,
        lambda: ingest_knowledge_base(
            client=ret_svc.client,
            embedding_service=emb_svc,
            csv_file_path=settings.rag.csv_file_path,
            index_name=settings.rag.index_name,
            batch_size=settings.rag.batch_size,
        ),
    )

    LOGGER.info(
        "Knowledge base ingestion complete: %d / %d documents indexed.",
        result.successful,
        result.total_rows,
    )
    return result


# Static frontend (must be mounted LAST) ---------------------------------------
#
# Starlette matches routes/mounts in registration order. A mount at "/"
# registered before the API routes above would swallow every request
# (including /health and /api/v1/*) once the frontend build exists,
# making the entire API unreachable. Mounting it last, after every
# explicit route, ensures API paths are matched first and only unmatched
# paths fall through to the static file server.
_frontend_dir = Path(__file__).parent.parent / "frontend"
if _frontend_dir.is_dir():
    app.mount(
        "/",
        StaticFiles(directory=str(_frontend_dir), html=True),
        name="frontend",
    )



####################################################################################################

"""
Frontend package marker for the YONO Business RAG Chatbot.

The ``static/`` sub-directory contains the self-contained single-page
chatbot web application served by FastAPI's static-file mount.

####################################################################################################
FILE: src\dlh_ai\genai\application\frontend\app.js
####################################################################################################

/* Config -------------------------------------------------------------------- */
const API_BASE_URL   = "";                       // same-origin by default
const CHAT_ENDPOINT  = `${API_BASE_URL}/api/v1/chat`;
const TOP_K          = 5;
let   SESSION_ID     = "sess-" + crypto.randomUUID();
let   msgCount       = 0;
let   chatHistory    = [];   // [{role, text, ts}] for export

/* DOM refs ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
const messagesWrap  = document.getElementById("messages-wrap");
const inputEl       = document.getElementById("query-input");
const sendBtn       = document.getElementById("send-btn");
const suggestionsBar= document.getElementById("suggestions-bar");
const toastEl       = document.getElementById("toast");
const onlinePill    = document.getElementById("online-pill");
const onlineDot     = document.getElementById("online-dot");
const onlineLabel   = document.getElementById("online-label");
const msgCountBadge = document.getElementById("msg-count");
const sessionList   = document.getElementById("session-list");
const currentSessEl = document.getElementById("current-session");

/* Helpers -------------------------------------------------------------------- */
function esc(str) {
    return String(str)
        .replace(/&/g,"&amp;").replace(/</g,"&lt;")
        .replace(/>/g,"&gt;").replace(/"/g,"&quot;")
        .replace(/'/g,"&#39;").replace(/`/g,"&#96;");
}

function now() {
    return new Date().toLocaleTimeString("en-IN", { hour:"2-digit", minute:"2-digit" });
}

function scrollBottom() {
    messagesWrap.scrollTo({ top: messagesWrap.scrollHeight, behavior: "smooth" });
}

function showToast(msg) {
    toastEl.textContent = msg;
    toastEl.classList.add("show");
    setTimeout(() => toastEl.classList.remove("show"), 4500);
}

function setOnline(online) {
    const color = online ? "#22c55e" : "#ef4444";
    onlineDot.style.background = color;
    onlinePill.style.color = color;
    onlineLabel.textContent  = online ? "Connected" : "Disconnected";
}

function bumpMsgCount() {
    msgCount++;
    msgCountBadge.textContent = msgCount;
}

function updateSessionLabel(text) {
    if (msgCount === 1) {
        const short = text.length > 28 ? text.slice(0, 28) + "…" : text;
        currentSessEl.textContent = short;
    }
}

/* Nav click ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
function navClick(el, section) {
    document.querySelectorAll(".nav-item").forEach(n => n.classList.remove("active"));
    el.classList.add("active");
    // Only chat section is functional in this release.
}

/* Auto-grow textarea ---------------------------------------------------------- */
inputEl.addEventListener("input", () => {
    inputEl.style.height = "auto";
    inputEl.style.height = Math.min(inputEl.scrollHeight, 130) + "px";
});

/* Append user message ---------------------------------------------------------- */
function appendUserMessage(text) {
    const ts = now();
    chatHistory.push({ role: "user", text, ts });
    bumpMsgCount();
    updateSessionLabel(text);

    const row = document.createElement("div");
    row.className = "msg-row user";
    row.innerHTML = `
        <div class="msg-body">
            <div class="msg-meta">
                <span class="msg-name">You</span>
                <span>${ts}</span>
            </div>
            <div class="bubble">${esc(text)}</div>
        </div>
        <div class="msg-avatar">U</div>`;
    messagesWrap.appendChild(row);
    scrollBottom();
}

/* Typing indicator ------------------------------------------------------------ */
function showTyping() {
    const row = document.createElement("div");
    row.className = "msg-row bot";
    row.id = "typing-row";
    row.innerHTML = `
        <div class="msg-avatar">AI</div>
        <div class="msg-body">
            <div class="msg-meta"><span class="msg-name">YONO Assistant</span></div>
            <div class="bubble">
                <span class="typing-dot"></span>
                <span class="typing-dot"></span>
                <span class="typing-dot"></span>
            </div>
        </div>`;
    messagesWrap.appendChild(row);
    scrollBottom();
}
function removeTyping() {
    const el = document.getElementById("typing-row");
    if (el) el.remove();
}

/* Append bot message ---------------------------------------------------------- */
function appendBotMessage(data) {
    const ts = now();
    chatHistory.push({ role: "assistant", text: data.answer, ts });
    bumpMsgCount();

    const pct = Math.round(data.score * 100);
    const srcId = "src-" + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8);

    let footerHtml = `
        <div class="bubble-footer">
            <span class="tag">${esc(data.category)}</span>
            <span class="tag">${esc(data.doc_type)}</span>
            <div class="confidence-bar-wrap">
                <span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--text-muted)">Confidence</span>
                <div class="confidence-bar">
                    <div class="confidence-fill" style="width:${pct}%"></div>
                </div>
                <span style="font-size:10px;font-weight:600;color:var(--tag-text)">${pct}%</span>
            </div>
        </div>`;

    let srcToggle = "";
    let srcBody   = "";
    if (data.sources && data.sources.length > 1) {
        const cards = data.sources.map((s, i) => `
            <div class="source-card">
                <strong>[${i+1}] ${esc(s.category)}</strong> &middot; ${esc(s.doc_type)}
                &middot; Sno: <strong>${esc(String(s.sno))}</strong>
                &middot; Score: <strong style="color:var(--tag-text)">${s.score.toFixed(4)}</strong><br/>
                <span style="color:var(--text-muted)">Q:</span> ${esc(s.question)}
            </div>`).join("");
        srcToggle = `<button class="sources-toggle" data-id="${srcId}">
            <svg width="10" height="10" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.5">
                <polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9"/>
            </svg>
            Show ${data.sources.length} source documents
        </button>`;
        srcBody = `<div class="sources-list hidden" id="${srcId}">${cards}</div>`;
    }

    let tableLinkHtml = "";
    if (data.source_table_link) {
        tableLinkHtml = `<a class="source-table-link" href="${esc(data.source_table_link)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">View source row in table &rarr;</a>`;
    }

    const row = document.createElement("div");
    row.className = "msg-row bot";
    row.innerHTML = `
        <div class="msg-avatar">AI</div>
        <div class="msg-body">
            <div class="msg-meta">
                <span class="msg-name">YONO Assistant</span>
                <span>${ts}</span>
            </div>
            <div class="bubble">
                ${esc(data.answer)}
                ${footerHtml}
                ${srcToggle}
                ${srcBody}
                ${tableLinkHtml}
            </div>
        </div>`;
    messagesWrap.appendChild(row);
    scrollBottom();

    /* sources toggle */
    row.querySelectorAll(".sources-toggle").forEach(btn => {
        btn.addEventListener("click", () => {
            const list = document.getElementById(btn.dataset.id);
            if (!list) return;
            const open = list.classList.toggle("hidden");
            const icon = btn.querySelector("svg polyline");
            if (icon) icon.setAttribute("points", open ? "6 9 12 15 18 9" : "6 15 12 9 18 15");
            btn.childNodes[1] && (btn.lastChild.textContent =
                (open ? " Show " : " Hide ") + data.sources.length + " source documents");
        });
    });
}

/* Append error message ---------------------------------------------------------- */
function appendErrorMessage(text) {
    const row = document.createElement("div");
    row.className = "msg-row bot";
    row.innerHTML = `
        <div class="msg-avatar" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#7f1d1d,#991b1b)">!</div>
        <div class="msg-body">
            <div class="msg-meta"><span class="msg-name">System</span><span>${now()}</span></div>
            <div class="bubble bubble-error">${esc(text)}</div>
        </div>`;
    messagesWrap.appendChild(row);
    scrollBottom();
}

/* Send message ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
async function sendMessage(queryText) {
    const query = (queryText || inputEl.value).trim();
    if (!query) return;

    suggestionsBar.style.display = "none";
    appendUserMessage(query);
    inputEl.value = "";
    inputEl.style.height = "auto";
    sendBtn.disabled = true;

    showTyping();

    try {
        const res = await fetch(CHAT_ENDPOINT, {
            method: "POST",
            headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
            body: JSON.stringify({ query, top_k: TOP_K, session_id: SESSION_ID }),
        });

        removeTyping();

        if (!res.ok) {
            const body = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
            console.error("Chat request failed:", res.status, body);
            appendErrorMessage(
                "Something went wrong on our end. Please try again in a moment, " +
                "or contact the YONO helpline at 1800-1234 (toll-free, 24×7)."
            );
            showToast("Request failed - please try again.");
            setOnline(false);
        } else {
            const data = await res.json();
            appendBotMessage(data);
            setOnline(true);
        }
    } catch (_) {
        removeTyping();
        appendErrorMessage(
            "Unable to reach the server. Check your connection or try again shortly."
        );
        showToast("Network error.");
        setOnline(false);
    } finally {
        sendBtn.disabled = false;
        inputEl.focus();
    }
}

/* Clear chat ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
function clearChat() {
    messagesWrap.innerHTML = "";
    const divider = document.createElement("div");
    divider.className = "date-divider";
    divider.textContent = "Today";
    messagesWrap.appendChild(divider);
    chatHistory = [];
    msgCount = 0;
    msgCountBadge.textContent = "0";
    SESSION_ID = "sess-" + crypto.randomUUID();
    suggestionsBar.style.display = "flex";
    currentSessEl.textContent = "New conversation";
    greet();
}

/* Export chat ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
function exportChat() {
    if (chatHistory.length === 0) { showToast("No messages to export."); return; }
    const lines = chatHistory.map(m =>
        `[${m.ts}] ${m.role === "user" ? "You" : "YONO Assistant"}: ${m.text}`
    );
    const blob = new Blob([lines.join("\n\n")], { type: "text/plain" });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement("a");
    a.href = url;
    a.download = `yono-chat-${Date.now()}.txt`;
    a.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
}

/* Event listeners --------------------------------------------------------------- */
sendBtn.addEventListener("click", () => sendMessage());

inputEl.addEventListener("keydown", e => {
    if (e.key === "Enter" && !e.shiftKey) { e.preventDefault(); sendMessage(); }
});

document.querySelectorAll(".chip").forEach(chip => {
    chip.addEventListener("click", () => sendMessage(chip.textContent.trim()));
});

/* Greeting ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
function greet() {
    const ts = now();
    const row = document.createElement("div");
    row.className = "msg-row bot";
    row.innerHTML = `
        <div class="msg-avatar">AI</div>
        <div class="msg-body">
            <div class="msg-meta">
                <span class="msg-name">YONO Assistant</span>
                <span>${ts}</span>
            </div>
            <div class="bubble">
                <strong>Welcome to YONO Business Assistant</strong>,
                State Bank of India&rsquo;s AI-powered banking helpdesk.<br/><br/>
                I can assist you with <strong>ATM &amp; Debit Card</strong> services,
                <strong>fund transfers</strong>, <strong>account enquiries</strong>,
                <strong>Fixed Deposits</strong>, <strong>loan details</strong>,
                and all other YONO features.<br/><br/>
                <em style="color:var(--text-muted)">Select a topic below or type your question.</em>
            </div>
        </div>`;
    messagesWrap.appendChild(row);
}

greet();
