                "RetrievalService.search_hybrid() received an empty query string."
            )
        if top_k < 1:
            raise ValueError(f"'top_k' must be >= 1, but received {top_k}.")

        query_vector: list[float] = self.embedding_service.encode_query(query)

        LOGGER.debug(
            "Executing hybrid search on index '%s' (top_k=%d).", self.index_name, top_k
        )

        try:
            raw_response = self.client.search(
                index=self.index_name,
                size=top_k,
                knn={
                    "field": "combined_text_embedding",
                    "query_vector": query_vector,
                    "k": top_k,
                    "num_candidates": max(top_k * 10, 50),
                    "boost": vector_boost,
                },
                query={
                    "multi_match": {
                        "query": query,
                        "fields": ["question^2", "title^1.5", "answer", "keywords", "category"],
                        "boost": bm25_boost,
                    }
                },
                source=_SOURCE_FIELDS,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            raise ElasticsearchSearchError(
                f"Hybrid search on index '{self.index_name}' failed: "
                f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
            ) from exc

        return self._parse_response(query=query, raw_response=raw_response)

    # Primary RAG answer method

    def answer_query(self, request: ChatRequest) -> ChatResponse:
        """Execute the full RAG pipeline: retrieve → prompt → LLM → respond.

        Steps:

        1. **Input validation**: if a :attr:`guardrail_service` is configured,
           the request is passed through PII detection and prompt-injection rails
           before any retrieval happens. If ``request.session_id`` is empty, a
           server-generated 12-digit session ID is assigned.
        2. Run kNN vector search to retrieve knowledge-base passages. If a
           :attr:`reranker_service` is configured and enabled, a wider
           candidate pool is retrieved and re-scored by the cross-encoder,
           truncated back down to ``request.top_k``.
        3. Load prior turns for this session (if :attr:`chat_history_service`
           is configured and enabled) and render the ``system`` and
           ``rag_chat`` prompt templates with the retrieved context and that
           history using :class:`~dlh_ai.genai.prompt.PromptManager`.
        4. Call :meth:`~LlmService.generate` to synthesise a coherent answer.
        5. **Output validation**: if a :attr:`guardrail_service` is configured,
           the response is passed through hallucination detection, PII leakage
           check, and regulatory-disclaimer enforcement before delivery.
        6. Return a :class:`ChatResponse` with the LLM answer and all source
           documents for transparency. If :attr:`chat_history_service` is
           configured and enabled, the (query, answer) turn is saved for
           this session.

        Parameters
        ----------
        request : ChatRequest
            Validated incoming chat request from the API layer.

        Returns
        -------
        ChatResponse
            Response containing the LLM-synthesised answer, top-hit metadata
            (category, doc_type, score), and all retrieved source documents.

        Raises
        ------
        GuardrailViolationError
            Raised when the guardrail service blocks the input or output.
        UnsupportedLanguageError
            Raised when the query is written in a non-English script.
        ClarificationNeededError
            Raised when the query is too short or ambiguous to act on directly.
        PIIDetectedError
            Raised when PII is found and ``enable_pii_redaction`` is ``False``.
        RetrievalError
            Raised when no knowledge-base documents are found for the query,
            or when the Elasticsearch search call fails.
        LlmGenerationError
            Raised when the LLM endpoint fails to generate a completion, for
            example due to a network failure or an authentication error.

        Notes
        -----
        * The ``system`` prompt sets the YONO Assistant persona and policies.
        * The ``rag_chat`` prompt injects the retrieved context passages,
          prior conversational turns (when chat history is enabled), and
          the user's question for the LLM to answer.
        * The LLM answer replaces the raw ``top_hit.answer`` so the response
          is synthesised across all retrieved passages rather than returning
          a single verbatim FAQ entry.

        Examples
        --------
        >>> from dlh_ai.genai.rag._models import ChatRequest
        >>> req = ChatRequest(query="How do I activate my debit card?", top_k=5)
        >>> response = service.answer_query(req)
        >>> print(response.answer)
        To activate your ATM/Debit card, you can use the YONO app...
        """
        LOGGER.info(
            "Processing chat query (session=%s): '%s'",
            request.session_id,
            request.query[:100],
        )

        # Step 1: Input guardrail validation -----------------------------
        if self.guardrail_service is not None:
            request = self.guardrail_service.validate_input(request)

        # Assign a server-generated session ID when the caller has none, so
        # every response carries an ID regardless of whether chat-history
        # persistence is enabled.
        if not request.session_id:
            request.session_id = generate_session_id()

        # Step 2: Retrieve knowledge-base passages -----------------------
        # When a reranker is active, widen the vector-search candidate pool
        # so the cross-encoder has more than request.top_k hits to re-score,
        # then truncate back down to request.top_k after reranking.
        search_top_k = request.top_k
        if self.reranker_service is not None and self.reranker_service.enabled:
            search_top_k = request.top_k * self.reranker_service.candidate_multiplier

        try:
            result: VectorSearchResponse = self.search(
                query=request.query,
                top_k=search_top_k,
            )
        except ElasticsearchSearchError as exc:
            raise RetrievalError(
                f"Retrieval failed for query '{request.query[:80]}': {exc}"
            ) from exc

        if not result.hits:
            raise RetrievalError(
                f"No knowledge-base documents found for query: '{request.query[:80]}'. "
                "The index may be empty or the query does not match any document."
            )

        if self.reranker_service is not None:
            reranked_hits = self.reranker_service.rerank(
                request.query, result.hits, top_n=request.top_k
            )
            result = result.model_copy(
                update={"hits": reranked_hits, "total_hits": len(reranked_hits)}
            )

        top_hit: SearchHit = result.hits[0]

        LOGGER.info(
            "Retrieved %d hit(s). Top score: %.4f | Category: '%s'",
            result.total_hits,
            top_hit.score,
            top_hit.category,
        )

        # Step 3: Render prompts ------------------------------------------
        system_prompt: str = self.prompt_manager.get_prompt(
            _PROMPT_AGENT, "system"
        )

        # Build context_items list for the rag_chat template.
        context_items: list[dict] = [
            {
                "rank": rank,
                "score": hit.score,
                "category": hit.category,
                "doc_type": hit.doc_type,
                "question": hit.question,
                "answer": hit.answer,
            }
            for rank, hit in enumerate(result.hits, start=1)
        ]

        history: list[dict[str, str]] = (
            self.chat_history_service.load_history(request.session_id)
            if self.chat_history_service is not None
            else []
        )

        user_prompt: str = self.prompt_manager.get_prompt(
            _PROMPT_AGENT,
            "rag_chat",
            query=request.query,
            context_items=context_items,
            history=history,
        )

        # Step 4: Generate LLM answer --------------------------------------
        LOGGER.debug("Sending context + query to LLM for synthesis...")
        try:
            llm_answer: str = self.llm_service.generate(
                system_prompt=system_prompt,
                user_prompt=user_prompt,
            )
        except LlmGenerationError:
            # Re-raise so the FastAPI handler can return a clean 503.
            raise

        LOGGER.info(
            "LLM answer generated. Length: %d chars.", len(llm_answer)
        )

        # Step 5: Build intermediate response -------------------------------
        source_table_link: str | None = None
        if self.enable_source_table_link and self.source_table_url_template:
            try:
                source_table_link = self.source_table_url_template.format(
                    sno=top_hit.sno
                )
            except (KeyError, IndexError) as exc:
                LOGGER.warning(
                    "Invalid source_table_url_template %r, omitting link: %s",
                    self.source_table_url_template,
                    exc,
                )

        response = ChatResponse(
            query=request.query,
            answer=llm_answer,
            category=top_hit.category,
            doc_type=top_hit.doc_type,
            score=top_hit.score,
            sources=result.hits,
            session_id=request.session_id,
            source_table_link=source_table_link,
        )

        # Step 6: Output guardrail validation ------------------------------
        if self.guardrail_service is not None:
            response = self.guardrail_service.validate_output(
                response=response,
                retrieved_context=result.hits,
            )

        if self.chat_history_service is not None:
            self.chat_history_service.save_turn(
                request.session_id, request.query, response.answer
            )

        return response

    # Internal helpers

    def _parse_response(
        self,
        query: str,
        raw_response: dict[str, object],
    ) -> VectorSearchResponse:
        """Parse a raw Elasticsearch search response into typed objects.

        Parameters
        ----------
        query : str
            Original query string for context in the response.
        raw_response : dict
            Raw dictionary returned by ``client.search()``.

        Returns
        -------
        VectorSearchResponse
            Typed and validated search response.

        Raises
        ------
        RetrievalError
            Raised when the expected ``hits.hits`` key is missing from the
            raw response, indicating an unexpected Elasticsearch API structure.
        """
        try:
            outer_hits: dict[str, object] = raw_response.get("hits", {})  # type: ignore[assignment]
            raw_hits: list[dict[str, object]] = outer_hits.get("hits", [])  # type: ignore[assignment]
        except AttributeError as exc:
            raise RetrievalError(
                "Failed to parse Elasticsearch response: unexpected structure. "
                f"Expected a dict with 'hits.hits', got: {type(raw_response).__name__}"
            ) from exc

        hits: list[SearchHit] = []
        for raw_hit in raw_hits:
            source: dict = raw_hit.get("_source", {})
            score: float = float(raw_hit.get("_score", 0.0))
            hit = SearchHit(
                score=score,
                sno=int(source.get("sno", 0)),
                category=str(source.get("category", "")),
                question=str(source.get("question", "")),
                answer=str(source.get("answer", "")),
                doc_type=str(source.get("type", "")),
                document_id=source.get("document_id") or None,
                document_url=source.get("document_url") or None,
                ingestion_date=source.get("ingestion_date") or None,
                title=source.get("title") or None,
                keywords=source.get("keywords") or None,
                sub_category=source.get("sub_category") or None,
            )
            hits.append(hit)

        return VectorSearchResponse(
            query=query,
            total_hits=len(hits),
            hits=hits,
        )



####################################################################################################
FILE: src\dlh_ai\genai\rag\exceptions.py
####################################################################################################

"""
Custom exceptions for the YONO Business RAG chatbot pipeline.

This module defines a domain-specific exception hierarchy for all
failure scenarios that can occur during RAG pipeline execution:
embedding, ingestion, retrieval, Elasticsearch operations, and
configuration validation.

All exceptions preserve the original cause via exception chaining
so the full traceback is always available without exposing secrets.
"""

import logging

LOGGER = logging.getLogger("dlh_ai")


# Base


class YonoRagError(Exception):
    """Base exception for all YONO RAG pipeline errors.

    All domain-specific exceptions inherit from this class so callers
    can catch the entire hierarchy with a single ``except YonoRagError``.

    Parameters
    ----------
    message : str
        Human-readable description of the failure.

    Examples
    --------
    >>> raise YonoRagError("Unexpected RAG pipeline failure.")
    """


# Configuration


class RagConfigurationError(YonoRagError):
    """Raised when the RAG pipeline configuration is invalid or incomplete.

    Parameters
    ----------
    message : str
        Description of which configuration field is invalid and why.

    Examples
    --------
    >>> raise RagConfigurationError(
    ...     "elasticsearch.host must not be empty."
    ... )
    """


# Elasticsearch


class ElasticsearchConnectionError(YonoRagError):
    """Raised when the Elasticsearch cluster cannot be reached.

    Parameters
    ----------
    message : str
        Context about the host and port that failed to respond.

    Examples
    --------
    >>> raise ElasticsearchConnectionError(
    ...     "Cannot connect to Elasticsearch at 'https://host:9201'. "
    ...     "Check network connectivity and cluster health."
    ... )
    """


class ElasticsearchIndexError(YonoRagError):
    """Raised when creating, deleting, or querying an Elasticsearch index fails.

    Parameters
    ----------
    message : str
        The index name and operation that failed.

    Examples
    --------
    >>> raise ElasticsearchIndexError(
    ...     "Failed to create index 'yono_business_knowledge_base': "
    ...     "mapping conflict on field 'combined_text_embedding'."
    ... )
    """


class ElasticsearchIngestionError(YonoRagError):
    """Raised when bulk document ingestion into Elasticsearch fails partially or entirely.

    Parameters
    ----------
    message : str
        Number of failed documents and reason for failure.

    Examples
    --------
    >>> raise ElasticsearchIngestionError(
    ...     "Bulk ingestion batch 3/11 failed: "
    ...     "5 documents rejected due to mapping errors."
    ... )
    """


class ElasticsearchSearchError(YonoRagError):
    """Raised when a kNN or hybrid search query against Elasticsearch fails.

    Parameters
    ----------
    message : str
        The query context and reason for the search failure.

    Examples
    --------
    >>> raise ElasticsearchSearchError(
    ...     "kNN search on index 'yono_business_knowledge_base' failed: "
    ...     "index does not contain a dense_vector field."
    ... )
    """


# Embedding


class EmbeddingModelError(YonoRagError):
    """Raised when the sentence-transformer embedding model cannot be loaded.

    Parameters
    ----------
    message : str
        The HDFS model path and reason the load failed.

    Examples
    --------
    >>> raise EmbeddingModelError(
    ...     "Failed to load SentenceTransformer model from HDFS path "
    ...     "'/dev/aiml/models/sentence-transformers/all-MiniLM-L12-v2': "
    ...     "HDFS path does not exist."
    ... )
    """


class EmbeddingDimensionMismatchError(YonoRagError):
    """Raised when the loaded model's embedding dimension differs from the configured value.

    Parameters
    ----------
    message : str
        Expected and actual dimensions.

    Examples
    --------
    >>> raise EmbeddingDimensionMismatchError(
    ...     "Expected embedding dimension 384, but model returned 768. "
    ...     "Verify 'rag.embedding_dimension' in config.toml matches the model."
    ... )
    """


class EmbeddingEncodingError(YonoRagError):
    """Raised when encoding a batch of texts into embeddings fails.

    Parameters
    ----------
    message : str
        Batch index and underlying cause of the encoding failure.

    Examples
    --------
    >>> raise EmbeddingEncodingError(
    ...     "Failed to encode batch starting at row 200: "
    ...     "CUDA out-of-memory error."
    ... )
    """


# Reranker


class RerankerModelError(YonoRagError):
    """Raised when the cross-encoder reranker model cannot be loaded.

    Parameters
    ----------
    message : str
        The model path and reason the load failed (tried both the ONNX
        Runtime backend and the torch ``CrossEncoder`` fallback).

    Examples
    --------
    >>> raise RerankerModelError(
    ...     "Failed to load reranker model from "
    ...     "'/dev/aiml/models/mixedbread-ai/mxbai-rerank-large-v2/': "
    ...     "HDFS path does not exist."
    ... )
    """


class RerankerEncodingError(YonoRagError):
    """Raised when scoring (query, passage) pairs with the reranker fails.

    Parameters
    ----------
    message : str
        Number of candidates being scored and the underlying cause.

    Examples
    --------
    >>> raise RerankerEncodingError(
    ...     "Failed to rerank 20 candidate(s): RuntimeError - "
    ...     "CUDA out-of-memory error."
    ... )
    """


# Ingestion


class KnowledgeBaseIngestionError(YonoRagError):
    """Raised when reading or validating the knowledge-base CSV fails.

    Parameters
    ----------
    message : str
        File path and reason the ingestion failed.

    Examples
    --------
    >>> raise KnowledgeBaseIngestionError(
    ...     "Failed to read knowledge-base CSV from "
    ...     "'yono_business_knowledge_base.csv': file not found."
    ... )
    """


# Retrieval / Chat


class RetrievalError(YonoRagError):
    """Raised when the RAG retrieval pipeline cannot return results for a query.

    Parameters
    ----------
    message : str
        Query context and failure reason.

    Examples
    --------
    >>> raise RetrievalError(
    ...     "Retrieval failed for query 'How do I activate my debit card?': "
    ...     "Elasticsearch returned an HTTP 503 response."
    ... )
    """


# LLM provider


class LlmLoadError(YonoRagError):
    """Raised when the LLM provider client cannot be initialised.

    Parameters
    ----------
    message : str
        The provider name, base URL, and reason the load failed.
        API keys must never appear in this message.

    Examples
    --------
    >>> raise LlmLoadError(
    ...     "Failed to initialise DLH OpenAI-compatible client "
    ...     "at 'http://10.190.236.15:9000/api/v1': connection refused."
    ... )
    """


class LlmGenerationError(YonoRagError):
    """Raised when the LLM endpoint fails to generate a completion.

    Covers network failures, non-2xx HTTP responses from the LLM server,
    and unexpected response body structures.

    Parameters
    ----------
    message : str
        Provider name, HTTP status (if available), and failure reason.
        Sensitive credentials must never be included.

    Examples
    --------
    >>> raise LlmGenerationError(
    ...     "LLM endpoint 'dlh' returned HTTP 503. "
    ...     "The model server is overloaded. Retry after a short delay."
    ... )
    """


# Guardrails


class GuardrailViolationError(YonoRagError):
    """Raised when user input or LLM output fails guardrail validation.

    This covers prompt-injection attempts, banking-context violations,
    and hallucinated (context-ungrounded) answers detected during output
    validation.

    Parameters
    ----------
    message : str
        Description of which rail was violated and why.

    Examples
    --------
    >>> raise GuardrailViolationError(
    ...     "Input blocked by guardrails (possible prompt injection)"
    ... )
    """


class PIIDetectedError(YonoRagError):
    """Raised when PII is detected in a user query and redaction is disabled.

    When ``GuardrailService.enable_pii_redaction`` is ``False`` this
    exception is raised instead of silently anonymising the input, allowing
    the caller to reject the request explicitly.

    Parameters
    ----------
    message : str
        List of detected PII entity types.

    Examples
    --------
    >>> raise PIIDetectedError(
    ...     "PII detected in query: ['IN_PAN', 'PHONE_NUMBER']"
    ... )
    """


class UnsupportedLanguageError(GuardrailViolationError):
    """Raised when a user query is written in a non-English script.

    Subclasses :class:`GuardrailViolationError` so existing
    ``except GuardrailViolationError`` call sites still catch this case.

    Examples
    --------
    >>> raise UnsupportedLanguageError(
    ...     "I currently support queries only in English. Support for "
    ...     "Hindi (Devanagari) will be added soon."
    ... )
    """


class ClarificationNeededError(GuardrailViolationError):
    """Raised when a user query is too short or ambiguous to act on directly.

    Subclasses :class:`GuardrailViolationError` so existing
    ``except GuardrailViolationError`` call sites still catch this case.

    Examples
    --------
    >>> raise ClarificationNeededError(
    ...     "Could you provide a bit more detail about what you'd like to know?"
    ... )
    """


class ProfanityDetectedError(GuardrailViolationError):
    """Raised when a user query contains profane language.

    Subclasses :class:`GuardrailViolationError` so existing
    ``except GuardrailViolationError`` call sites still catch this case.

    Examples
    --------
    >>> raise ProfanityDetectedError("Input blocked: profane language detected")
    """


class ToxicContentError(GuardrailViolationError):
    """Raised when a user query is classified as toxic by the toxicity model.

    Subclasses :class:`GuardrailViolationError` so existing
    ``except GuardrailViolationError`` call sites still catch this case.

    Examples
    --------
    >>> raise ToxicContentError(
    ...     "Input blocked: toxicity score 0.92 exceeds threshold 0.70"
    ... )
    """


class PromptInjectionDetectedError(GuardrailViolationError):
    """Raised when a user query matches a prompt-injection / role-override pattern.

    Subclasses :class:`GuardrailViolationError` so existing
    ``except GuardrailViolationError`` call sites still catch this case.

    Examples
    --------
    >>> raise PromptInjectionDetectedError(
    ...     "Input blocked by guardrails (possible prompt injection or "
    ...     "role-override attempt)"
    ... )
    """


class OutOfDomainError(GuardrailViolationError):
    """Raised when a user query has no banking/YONO-domain relevance.

    Subclasses :class:`GuardrailViolationError` so existing
    ``except GuardrailViolationError`` call sites still catch this case.

    Examples
    --------
    >>> raise OutOfDomainError(
    ...     "Input blocked by guardrails (out-of-domain request, please "
    ...     "ask a YONO banking-related question)"
    ... )
    """


class HallucinationDetectedError(GuardrailViolationError):
    """Raised when a generated answer is not grounded in the retrieved context.

    Subclasses :class:`GuardrailViolationError` so existing
    ``except GuardrailViolationError`` call sites still catch this case.

    Examples
    --------
    >>> raise HallucinationDetectedError(
    ...     "Generated answer contains claims not grounded in retrieved "
    ...     "context (grounding score 0.41 below threshold 0.60)"
    ... )
    """


class InvalidCitationError(GuardrailViolationError):
    """Raised when a generated answer cites a source with no genuine relevance.

    Subclasses :class:`GuardrailViolationError` so existing
    ``except GuardrailViolationError`` call sites still catch this case.

    Examples
    --------
    >>> raise InvalidCitationError(
    ...     "Generated answer cites source(s) with no genuine relevance "
    ...     "to the query"
    ... )
    """

    "                \"Keeping existing index.\"\n",
    "            )\n",

    "\n",
    "    logger.info(\n",
    "        \"Creating Elasticsearch index '%s'...\",\n",
    "        index_name,\n",
    "    )\n",
    "\n",
    "    client.indices.create(\n",
    "        index=index_name,\n",
    "        mappings=mappings,\n",
    "    )\n",
    "\n",
    "    logger.info(\n",
    "        \"Index '%s' created successfully \"\n",
    "        \"with %s-dimensional vectors.\",\n",
    "        index_name,\n",
    "        EMBEDDING_DIMENSION,\n",

    "):\n",
    "    \"\"\"\n",
    "    Read CSV, generate embeddings and bulk ingest\n",
    "    documents into Elasticsearch.\n",
    "\n",
    "    tqdm is used to show ingestion progress.\n",
    "    \"\"\"\n",
    "\n",
    "    logger.info(\n",
    "        \"Reading CSV: %s\",\n",
    "        csv_file_path,\n",
    "    )\n",
    "\n",
    "    df = pd.read_csv(\n",
    "        csv_file_path\n",
    "    )\n",
    "\n",
    "    logger.info(\n",
    "        \"CSV loaded. Total rows: %s\",\n",
    "        len(df),\n",
    "    )\n",
    "\n",
    "    # Validate required columns\n",
    "\n",
    "    required_columns = [\n",
    "        \"Sno\",\n",
    "        \"Category\",\n",
    "        \"Question\",\n",
    "        \"Answer\",\n",
    "        \"Type\",\n",
    "    ]\n",
    "\n",
    "    missing_columns = [\n",
    "        column\n",
    "        for column in required_columns\n",
    "        if column not in df.columns\n",
    "    ]\n",
    "\n",
    "    if missing_columns:\n",
    "\n",
    "        raise ValueError(\n",
    "            f\"Missing CSV columns: \"\n",
    "            f\"{missing_columns}\"\n",
    "        )\n",
    "\n",
    "    # Fill missing values\n",
    "\n",
    "    df[\"Category\"] = (\n",
    "        df[\"Category\"]\n",
    "        .ffill()\n",
    "        .fillna(\"\")\n",
    "    )\n",
    "\n",
    "    df[\"Question\"] = (\n",
    "        df[\"Question\"]\n",
    "        .fillna(\"\")\n",
    "    )\n",
    "\n",
    "    df[\"Answer\"] = (\n",
    "        df[\"Answer\"]\n",
    "        .fillna(\"\")\n",
    "    )\n",
    "\n",
    "    df[\"Type\"] = (\n",
    "        df[\"Type\"]\n",
    "        .fillna(\"\")\n",
    "    )\n",
    "\n",
    "    # Process in batches\n",
    "\n",
    "    total_rows = len(df)\n",
    "\n",
    "    successful = 0\n",
    "    failed = 0\n",
    "\n",
    "    for start in tqdm(\n",
    "        range(\n",
    "            0,\n",
    "            total_rows,\n",
    "            batch_size,\n",
    "        ),\n",
    "        desc=\"Ingesting CSV\",\n",
    "        unit=\"batch\",\n",
    "    ):\n",
    "\n",
    "        batch_df = df.iloc[\n",
    "            start : start + batch_size\n",
    "        ]\n",
    "\n",
    "        # Create combined text\n",
    "\n",
    "        texts = [\n",
    "            create_combined_text(row)\n",
    "            for _, row in batch_df.iterrows()\n",
    "        ]\n",
    "\n",
    "        # Generate embeddings\n",
    "\n",
    "        embeddings = model.encode(\n",
    "            texts,\n",
    "            normalize_embeddings=True,\n",
    "            show_progress_bar=False,\n",
    "        )\n",
    "\n",
    "        # Create Elasticsearch bulk actions\n",
    "\n",
    "        actions = []\n",
    "\n",
    "        for (_, row), embedding, combined_text in zip(\n",
    "            batch_df.iterrows(),\n",
    "            embeddings,\n",
    "            texts,\n",
    "        ):\n",
    "\n",
    "            # Clean category\n",
    "\n",
    "            category = str(\n",
    "                row[\"Category\"]\n",
    "            ).strip()\n",
    "\n",
    "            # Elasticsearch document\n",
    "\n",
    "            doc = {\n",
    "                \"sno\": int(row[\"Sno\"]),\n",
    "                \"category\": category,\n",
    "                \"question\": str(\n",
    "                    row[\"Question\"]\n",
    "                ).strip(),\n",
    "                \"answer\": str(\n",
    "                    row[\"Answer\"]\n",
    "                ).strip(),\n",
    "                \"type\": str(\n",
    "                    row[\"Type\"]\n",
    "                ).strip(),\n",
    "                \"combined_text\": combined_text,\n",
    "                \"combined_text_embedding\": (\n",
    "                    embedding.tolist()\n",
    "                ),\n",
    "            }\n",
    "\n",
    "            actions.append(\n",
    "                {\n",
    "                    \"_index\": index_name,\n",
    "                    \"_source\": doc,\n",
    "                }\n",
    "            )\n",
    "\n",
    "        # Bulk insert\n",
    "\n",
    "        try:\n",
    "\n",
    "            success, errors = helpers.bulk(\n",
    "                client,\n",
    "                actions,\n",
    "                raise_on_error=False,\n",
    "            )\n",
    "\n",
    "            successful += success\n",
    "\n",
    "            if errors:\n",
    "                failed += len(errors)\n",
    "\n",
    "        except Exception as e:\n",
    "\n",
    "            logger.exception(\n",
    "                \"Bulk ingestion failed: %s\",\n",
    "                e,\n",
    "            )\n",
    "\n",
    "            failed += len(actions)\n",
    "\n",
    "    # Final statistics\n",
    "\n",
    "    logger.info(\n",
    "        \"Ingestion completed.\"\n",
    "    )\n",
    "\n",
    "    logger.info(\n",
    "        \"Successfully indexed: %s\",\n",
    "        successful,\n",
    "    )\n",
    "\n",
    "    logger.info(\n",
    "        \"Failed: %s\",\n",
    "        failed,\n",
    "    )\n",
    "\n",
    "    return successful, failed\n"
   ]
  },
  {
   "cell_type": "markdown",
   "id": "3e776429-d75e-406c-99ec-0787522a84dd",
   "metadata": {},
   "source": [
    "# 7. SIMPLE VECTOR SEARCH"
   ]
  },
  {
   "cell_type": "code",
   "execution_count": 26,
   "id": "48062de5-b910-4c72-8aa3-6deca2784dc0",
   "metadata": {},
   "outputs": [],
   "source": [
    "def vector_search(\n",
    "    client: Elasticsearch,\n",
    "    model,\n",
    "    index_name: str,\n",
    "    query: str,\n",
    "    top_k: int = 5,\n",
    "):\n",
    "    \"\"\"\n",
    "    Perform simple Elasticsearch kNN vector search.\n",
    "    \"\"\"\n",
    "\n",
    "    # Generate query embedding\n",
    "\n",
    "    query_embedding = model.encode(\n",
    "        query,\n",
    "        normalize_embeddings=True,\n",
    "    ).tolist()\n",
    "\n",
    "    # Elasticsearch kNN search\n",
    "\n",
    "    response = client.search(\n",
    "        index=index_name,\n",
    "        knn={\n",
    "            \"field\": \"combined_text_embedding\",\n",
    "            \"query_vector\": query_embedding,\n",
    "            \"k\": top_k,\n",
    "            \"num_candidates\": max(\n",
    "                top_k * 10,\n",
    "                50,\n",
    "            ),\n",
    "        },\n",
    "        source=[\n",
    "            \"sno\",\n",
    "            \"category\",\n",
    "            \"question\",\n",
    "            \"answer\",\n",
    "            \"type\",\n",
    "            \"combined_text\",\n",
    "        ],\n",
    "    )\n",
    "\n",
    "    return response"
   ]
  },
  {
   "cell_type": "markdown",
   "id": "f60263d8-7c6e-4195-b1c4-c7b540b7e366",
   "metadata": {},
   "source": [
    "# 8. DISPLAY VECTOR SEARCH RESULTS"
   ]
  },
  {
   "cell_type": "code",
   "execution_count": null,
   "id": "5c2dc297-b62c-4d24-a6d0-9d00ecc1c77d",
   "metadata": {},
   "outputs": [],
   "source": [
    "def display_search_results(\n",
    "    response,\n",
    "):\n",
    "    \"\"\"\n",
    "    Display Elasticsearch vector search\n",
    "    results in a readable format.\n",
    "    \"\"\"\n",
    "\n",
    "    hits = response.get(\n",
    "        \"hits\",\n",
    "        {}\n",
    "    ).get(\n",
    "        \"hits\",\n",
    "        []\n",
    "    )\n",
    "\n",
    "    if not hits:\n",
    "\n",
    "        display(\n",
    "            Markdown(\n",
    "                \"### No results found.\"\n",
    "            )\n",
    "        )\n",
    "\n",
    "        return\n",
    "\n",
    "    display(\n",
    "        Markdown(\n",
    "            f\"### Found {len(hits)} results\"\n",
    "        )\n",
    "    )\n",
    "\n",
    "    for rank, hit in enumerate(\n",
    "        hits,\n",
    "        start=1,\n",
    "    ):\n",
    "\n",
    "        source = hit.get(\n",
    "            \"_source\",\n",
    "            {}\n",
    "        )\n",
    "\n",
    "        score = hit.get(\n",
    "            \"_score\"\n",
    "        )\n",
    "\n",
    "        sno = source.get(\n",
    "            \"sno\",\n",
    "            \"\"\n",
    "        )\n",
    "\n",
    "        category = source.get(\n",
    "            \"category\",\n",
    "            \"\"\n",
    "        )\n",
    "\n",
    "        question = source.get(\n",
    "            \"question\",\n",
    "            \"\"\n",
    "        )\n",
    "\n",
    "        answer = source.get(\n",
    "            \"answer\",\n",
    "            \"\"\n",
    "        )\n",
    "\n",
    "        doc_type = source.get(\n",
    "            \"type\",\n",
    "            \"\"\n",
    "        )\n",
    "\n",
    "        display(\n",
    "            Markdown(\n",
    "                f\"\"\"\n",
    "### {rank}. Score: `{score:.4f}`\n",
    "\n",
    "**S.No:** {sno}\n",
    "\n",
    "**Category:** {category}\n",
    "\n",
    "**Type:** {doc_type}\n",
    "\n",
    "**Question:** {question}\n",
    "\n",
    "**Answer:** {answer}\n",
    "\n",
    "---\n",
    "\"\"\"\n",
    "            )\n",
    "        )\n",
    "\n"
   ]
  },

    "def create_search_ui(\n",
    "    client: Elasticsearch,\n",
    "    model,\n",
    "    index_name: str,\n",
    "):\n",
    "    \"\"\"\n",
    "    Create a simple interactive search UI\n",
    "    for Jupyter Notebook.\n",
    "    \"\"\"\n",
    "\n",
    "    query_box = widgets.Text(\n",
    "        value=\"\",\n",
    "        placeholder=(\n",
    "            \"Enter your question...\"\n",
    "        ),\n",
    "        description=\"Query:\",\n",
    "        layout=widgets.Layout(\n",
    "            width=\"80%\"\n",
    "        ),\n",
    "    )\n",
    "\n",
    "    top_k_box = widgets.IntSlider(\n",
    "        value=5,\n",
    "        min=1,\n",
    "        max=20,\n",
    "        step=1,\n",
    "        description=\"Top K:\",\n",
    "        continuous_update=False,\n",
    "    )\n",
    "\n",
    "    search_button = widgets.Button(\n",
    "        description=\"Search\",\n",
    "        button_style=\"primary\",\n",
    "        icon=\"search\",\n",
    "    )\n",
    "\n",
    "    output = widgets.Output()\n",
    "\n",
    "    def on_search(\n",
    "        button\n",
    "    ):\n",
    "\n",
    "        with output:\n",
    "\n",
    "            clear_output(\n",
    "                wait=True\n",
    "            )\n",
    "\n",
    "            query = (\n",
    "                query_box.value\n",
    "                .strip()\n",
    "            )\n",
    "\n",
    "            if not query:\n",
    "\n",
    "                display(\n",
    "                    Markdown(\n",
    "                        \"Please enter a query.\"\n",
    "                    )\n",
    "                )\n",
    "\n",
    "                return\n",
    "\n",
    "            display(\n",
    "                Markdown(\n",
    "                    f\"### Searching for:\\n\"\n",
    "                    f\"**{query}**\"\n",
    "                )\n",
    "            )\n",
    "\n",
    "            try:\n",
    "\n",
    "                response = vector_search(\n",
    "                    client=client,\n",
    "                    model=model,\n",
    "                    index_name=index_name,\n",
    "                    query=query,\n",
    "                    top_k=top_k_box.value,\n",
    "                )\n",
    "\n",
    "                display_search_results(\n",
    "                    response\n",
    "                )\n",
    "\n",
    "            except Exception as e:\n",
    "\n",
    "                display(\n",
    "                    Markdown(\n",
    "                        f\"**Search error:** `{e}`\"\n",
    "                    )\n",
    "                )\n",
    "\n",
    "    search_button.on_click(\n",
    "        on_search\n",
    "    )\n",
    "\n",
    "    display(\n",
    "        widgets.VBox(\n",
    "            [\n",
    "                query_box,\n",
    "                top_k_box,\n",
    "                search_button,\n",
    "                output,\n",
    "            ]\n",
    "        )\n",
    "    )\n"
   ]
  },

####################################################################################################
FILE: src\dlh_ai\utils\_cml_api.py
####################################################################################################


import logging
from datetime import datetime
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo

import cmlapi
from cmlapi import CMLServiceApi, CreateJobRequest, models

logger = logging.getLogger("dlh_ai")

RUNTIME_IDENTIFIER = [
    "hadoop-cli-7.3.1.200-90",
    # This equals to spark 3.5.1; to change spark version probably create dummy job with
    # respective spark version & then use CML Job api to fetch details
    "spark351-7218800-1240-b711",
]
DEFAULT_TIMEOUT = 60 * 10


class CAIClient:
    """Client for interacting with Cloudera AI (CAI) API.

    Parameters
    ----------
    project_id : str
        The CML project identifier.

    Examples
    --------
    >>> client = CAIClient(project_id="my-project-123")

    >>> job_config = client.job_config_template(
    ...     job_prefix_name="data-processing",
    ...     batch_no=1,
    ...     job_script="src/jobs/process_data.py"
    ... )
    >>> job = client.create_job(job_config)
    >>> job_run = client.create_job_run(job.id)
    """

    project_id: str

    @property
    def client(self) -> CMLServiceApi:
        """Get the CML service API client.

        Returns
        -------
        CMLServiceApi
            The default CML API client instance.

        Examples
        --------
        >>> cai = CAIClient()
        >>> api_client = cai.client
        """
        _ = cmlapi.default_client()
        return _

    def job_config_template(
        self,
        job_prefix_name: str,
        batch_no: int | None,
        job_script: str,
        cpu: int = 2,
        memory: int = 8,
        env: dict | None = None,
        runtime_identifier: str = "nexus.dlhdevuat.sbi.bank.in:9443/clouderadataservices/cloudera/cdsw/ml-runtime-jupyterlab-python3.11-ml:2026.03.03.v1",
    ) -> CreateJobRequest:
        """Create a CML job configuration template.

        Generates a job configuration with specified resources and runtime settings.
        If batch_no is provided, it will be included in the job name along with
        the current timestamp.

        Parameters
        ----------
        job_prefix_name : str
            Prefix for the job name.
        batch_no : int or None
            Batch number to include in job name. If None, only prefix is used.
        job_script : str
            Path to the script file to execute (e.g., "src/jobs/process_data.py").
        cpu : int, optional
            Number of CPU cores to allocate, by default 2.
        memory : int, optional
            Memory in GB to allocate, by default 8.
        env : dict or None, optional
            Environment variables as key-value pairs, by default None.
        runtime_identifier : str, optional
            Docker image identifier for the runtime environment.

        Returns
        -------
        CreateJobRequest
            Configured job request object ready for submission.

        Examples
        --------
        >>> client = CAIClient(project_id="proj-123")
        >>> # Create job config without batch number
        >>> config = client.job_config_template(
        ...     job_prefix_name="etl-pipeline",
        ...     batch_no=None,
        ...     job_script="src/jobs/etl.py",
        ...     cpu=4,
        ...     memory=16
        ... )
        >>>
        >>> # Create job config with batch number
        >>> config = client.job_config_template(
        ...     job_prefix_name="data-processing",
        ...     batch_no=5,
        ...     job_script="src/jobs/process.py",
        ...     env={"ENV": "production", "LOG_LEVEL": "INFO"}
        ... )

        Notes
        -----
        - The job timeout is set to 10 minutes (600 seconds).
        - Jobs are configured to be killed on timeout.
        - Spark runtime addons are automatically included for Spark 3.5.1.
        """
        # If there are batches then only we'll have batch name in job name
        name = (
            f"{job_prefix_name}-batch-{batch_no}-{str(datetime.now(tz=ZoneInfo('Asia/Kolkata')))}"
            if batch_no
            else job_prefix_name
        )

        return cmlapi.CreateJobRequest(
            name=name,
            project_id=self.project_id,
            script=job_script,
            cpu=cpu,
            memory=memory,
            environment=env,
            # NOTE - runtime should match with intended runtime docker image
            runtime_identifier=runtime_identifier,
            runtime_addon_identifiers=RUNTIME_IDENTIFIER,
            timeout=DEFAULT_TIMEOUT,
            kill_on_timeout=True,
        )

    def create_job(self, job_config: CreateJobRequest) -> models.job.Job:
        """Create a new CML job.

        Submits a job configuration to CML and creates the job in the project.

        Parameters
        ----------
        job_config : CreateJobRequest
            Job configuration object created by job_config_template().

        Returns
        -------
        models.job.Job
            The created job object with job ID and metadata.

        Examples
        --------
        >>> client = CAIClient(project_id="proj-123")

        >>> config = client.job_config_template(
        ...     job_prefix_name="ml-training",
        ...     batch_no=1,
        ...     job_script="src/jobs/train_model.py"
        ... )
        >>> job = client.create_job(config)
        >>> print(f"Job created with ID: {job.id}")
        Job created with ID: job-abc-123

        See Also
        --------
        job_config_template : Create job configuration
        create_job_run : Execute a created job
        """
        job = self.client.create_job(job_config, project_id=self.project_id)
        logger.info(f"successfully created job with {job.id}")
        return job

    def create_job_run(self, job_id: str) -> models.job_run.JobRun:
        """Execute a CML job by creating a job run.

        Creates and starts a new run for an existing job.

        Parameters
        ----------
        job_id : str
            The unique identifier of the job to run.

        Returns
        -------
        models.job_run.JobRun
            The created job run object with run ID and status.

        Examples
        --------
        >>> client = CAIClient(project_id="proj-123")
        >>> # First create a job
        >>> config = client.job_config_template(
        ...     job_prefix_name="data-pipeline",
        ...     batch_no=None,
        ...     job_script="src/jobs/pipeline.py"
        ... )
        >>> job = client.create_job(config)
        >>>
        >>> # Then execute the job
        >>> job_run = client.create_job_run(job.id)
        >>> print(f"Job run started with ID: {job_run.id}")
        Job run started with ID: run-xyz-456

        See Also
        --------
        create_job : Create a new job
        _job_run_template : Generate job run configuration
        """
        job_run_config = self._job_run_template(job_id=job_id)
        run = self.client.create_job_run(
            job_run_config, job_id=job_id, project_id=setting.common.project_id
        )
        logger.info(f"successfully created job run {run.id}")
        return run

    def _job_run_template(self, job_id: str):
        """Create a job run configuration template.

        Internal method to generate the configuration for executing a job.

        Parameters
        ----------
        job_id : str
            The unique identifier of the job to run.

        Returns
        -------
        cmlapi.CreateJobRunRequest
            Job run request configuration object.

        Examples
        --------
        >>> client = CAIClient(project_id="proj-123")
        >>> run_config = client._job_run_template(job_id="job-abc-123")
        """
        return cmlapi.CreateJobRunRequest(project_id=self.project_id, job_id=job_id)


####################################################################################################
FILE: src\dlh_ai\utils\_hdfs_client.py
####################################################################################################


"""
HDFS client for the YONO Business RAG pipeline.

Wraps PyArrow's ``HadoopFileSystem`` and the Hadoop CLI for all HDFS I/O
needed by the embedding and data-loading services.
"""

import logging
import os
import subprocess
import tempfile
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

import pyarrow.dataset as ds
import pyarrow.fs as fs

logger = logging.getLogger("dlh_ai")


@dataclass
class HDFSClient:
    """HDFS client for interacting with Hadoop Distributed File System.

    This client provides methods for file operations, data reading, and model
    loading from HDFS using PyArrow's filesystem interface and the Hadoop CLI.

    Parameters
    ----------
    host : str, default="default"
        HDFS host connection string.  Use ``"default"`` to connect to the
        cluster configured in ``core-site.xml`` / ``hdfs-site.xml``.

    Examples
    --------
    >>> client = HDFSClient()
    >>> files = client.list_files("/user/some_folder/data")
    >>> client.upload("local_file.csv", "/user/data/remote_file.csv")
    >>> client.download("/user/data/remote_file.csv", "local_file.csv")
    >>> dataset = client.read_parquet_table("/user/data/parquet_files")
    """

    host: str = "default"

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        logger.debug("Configuring Hadoop environment variables for HDFS client.")

        # Resolve the JVM class-path for the Hadoop native libraries.
        # subprocess.run is used instead of the deprecated os.popen() which
        # was removed from recommended usage in Python 3.12 (PEP 594 shadow).
        result = subprocess.run(
            ["hdfs", "classpath"],
            capture_output=True,
            text=True,
            check=False,
        )
        if result.returncode == 0:
            os.environ["CLASSPATH"] = result.stdout.strip()
        else:
            logger.warning(
                "Could not resolve HDFS classpath (exit=%d): %s",
                result.returncode,
                result.stderr.strip(),
            )

        # Cloudera cluster TLS trust-store, configurable via env so the
        # password is never hardcoded in committed source.
        truststore_path = os.environ.get(
            "HADOOP_TRUSTSTORE_PATH",
            "/var/lib/cloudera-scm-agent/agent-cert/cm-auto-global_truststore.jks",
        )
        truststore_pass = os.environ.get("HADOOP_TRUSTSTORE_PASSWORD", "")
        os.environ["HADOOP_OPTS"] = (
            f"-Djavax.net.ssl.trustStore={truststore_path} "
            f"-Djavax.net.ssl.trustStorePassword={truststore_pass}"
        )

    @property
    def hdfs_filesystem(self) -> fs.HadoopFileSystem:
        """Get PyArrow HadoopFileSystem instance.

        Returns
        -------
        fs.HadoopFileSystem
            Configured HadoopFileSystem instance for HDFS operations.

        Examples
        --------
        >>> client = HDFSClient()
        >>> filesystem = client.hdfs_filesystem
        >>> file_info = filesystem.get_file_info("/user/data")
        """
        config = {
            "ssl.client.truststore.location": "/var/lib/cloudera-scm-agent/agent-cert/cm-auto-global_truststore.jks",
            "ssl.client.truststore.password": "Sbi@123",
            "ssl.client.truststore.type": "jks",
            "hadoop.rpc.socket.factory.class.default": "org.apache.hadoop.net.StandardSocketFactory"
        }
        _ = fs.HadoopFileSystem(host=self.host, extra_conf=config)
        return _

    def list_files(self, path: str) -> list[fs.FileInfo]:
        """List all files in the specified HDFS path recursively.

        Parameters
        ----------
        path : str
            HDFS path to list files from.

        Returns
        -------
        list[fs.FileInfo]
            List of FileInfo objects containing file metadata.

        Examples
        --------
        >>> client = HDFSClient()
        >>> files = client.list_files("/user/data")
        >>> for file in files:
        ...     print(f"{file.path}: {file.size} bytes")
        """
        selector = fs.FileSelector(path, recursive=True)
        files = self.hdfs_filesystem.get_file_info(selector)
        return [file for file in files]

    def upload(self, source_path: str, destination_path: str, overwrite: bool = True):
        """Upload a file from local filesystem to HDFS.

        Parameters
        ----------
        source_path : str
            Local file path to upload.
        destination_path : str
            HDFS destination path.
        overwrite : bool, optional
            Overwrite existing file if it exists. Default is True.

        Examples
        --------
        >>> client = HDFSClient()
        >>> client.upload("local_data.csv", "/user/data/remote_data.csv")
        >>>
        >>> # Upload without force overwrite
        >>> client.upload("model.pkl", "/user/models/model.pkl", overwrite=False)

        Notes
        -----
        Uses HDFS CLI command with -f flag to force overwrite if file exists.
        PyArrow filesystem is not used due to authentication issues.
        """
        # TODO: switch to pyarrow.fs once the Kerberos auth issue is resolved.
        cmd = ["hdfs", "dfs", "-put"]
        if overwrite:
            cmd.append("-f")
        cmd += [source_path, destination_path]
        result = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, check=False)
        if result.returncode != 0:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"'hdfs dfs -put {source_path} {destination_path}' failed "
                f"with exit code {result.returncode}: {result.stderr.strip()}"
            )

    def download(self, source_path: str, destination_path: str):
        """Download a file from HDFS to local filesystem.

        Parameters
        ----------
        source_path : str
            HDFS file path to download.
        destination_path : str
            Local destination path.

        Raises
        ------
        RuntimeError
            Raised when the ``hdfs dfs -get`` command exits with a
            non-zero status (e.g. Kerberos auth failure, missing path).
            The underlying stderr output is included in the message.

        Examples
        --------
        >>> client = HDFSClient()
        >>> client.download("/user/data/remote_data.csv", "local_data.csv")
        >>>
        >>> # Download to specific directory
        >>> client.download("/user/models/model.pkl", "./models/model.pkl")

        Notes
        -----
        Uses HDFS CLI command for downloading.
        PyArrow filesystem is not used due to authentication issues.
        """
        # TODO - Ideally pyarrow.fs should be used but currently there is
        # a issue with auth & only hdfs cli is working
        result = subprocess.run(
            ["hdfs", "dfs", "-get", source_path, destination_path],
            capture_output=True,
            text=True,
        )
        if result.returncode != 0:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"'hdfs dfs -get {source_path} {destination_path}' failed "
                f"with exit code {result.returncode}: {result.stderr.strip()}"
            )

    def read_parquet_table(self, source_path: str) -> ds.FileSystemDataset:
        """Read parquet files from HDFS as a PyArrow dataset.

        Parameters
        ----------
        source_path : str
            HDFS path to parquet file or directory containing parquet files.

        Returns
        -------
        ds.FileSystemDataset
            PyArrow FileSystemDataset for the parquet data.

        Examples
        --------
        >>> client = HDFSClient()
        >>> dataset = client.read_parquet_table("/user/data/parquet_files")
        >>>
        >>> # Convert to pandas DataFrame
        >>> df = dataset.to_table().to_pandas()
        >>>
        >>> # Read with filters
        >>> table = dataset.to_table(filter=ds.field("year") == 2024)
        >>> df = table.to_pandas()
        """
        return ds.dataset(
            source=source_path, filesystem=self.hdfs_filesystem, format="parquet"
        )

    def read_csv_file(self, source_path: str) -> ds.FileSystemDataset:
        """Read CSV files from HDFS as a PyArrow dataset.

        Parameters
        ----------
        source_path : str
            HDFS path to CSV file or directory containing CSV files.

        Returns
        -------
        ds.FileSystemDataset
            PyArrow FileSystemDataset for the CSV data.

        Examples
        --------
        >>> client = HDFSClient()
        >>> dataset = client.read_csv_file("/user/data/csv_files")
        """
        return ds.dataset(
            source=source_path, filesystem=self.hdfs_filesystem, format="csv"
        )

    def load_model_sentence_transformer(self, model_path: str, **kwargs):
        """Load a SentenceTransformer model from HDFS.

        Downloads the model from HDFS to a temporary directory and loads it
        using the sentence-transformers library.

        Parameters
        ----------
        model_path : str
            HDFS path to the SentenceTransformer model directory.
        **kwargs : dict
            Additional keyword arguments to pass to SentenceTransformer constructor.

        Returns
        -------
        SentenceTransformer
            Loaded SentenceTransformer model instance.

        Raises
        ------
        ImportError
            If sentence-transformers library is not installed.
        ValueError
            If model requires trust_remote_code=True (automatically handled).

        Examples
        --------
        >>> client = HDFSClient()
        >>> model = client.load_model_sentence_transformer(
        ...     "/dev/aiml/models/google/embeddinggemma4"
        ... )
        >>> embeddings = model.encode(["Hello world", "How are you?"])

        >>> # Load with onnx device backend
        >>> model = client.load_model_sentence_transformer(
        ...     "/dev/aiml/models/google/embeddinggemma4",
        ...     backend="onnx"
        ... )

        Notes
        -----
        The model is downloaded to a temporary directory which is automatically
        cleaned up after loading. If the model requires trust_remote_code=True,
        it will be automatically enabled with a warning.
        """
        try:
            from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer
        except ImportError:
            raise ImportError(
                "`sentence-transformers` library is not installed, please install it"
            )

        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp_dir:
            local_model_path = f"{temp_dir}/model_dir"
            logger.debug("Downloading SentenceTransformer model from HDFS: %s", model_path)
            self.download(model_path, local_model_path)
            logger.debug("Model downloaded to temporary path: %s", local_model_path)
            try:
                logger.info("Loading SentenceTransformer from %s", local_model_path)
                model = SentenceTransformer(local_model_path, **kwargs)
            except ValueError as exc:
                if "trust_remote_code=True" in str(exc):
                    logger.warning(
                        "Model requires trust_remote_code=True, enabling automatically."
                    )
                    model = SentenceTransformer(
                        local_model_path, trust_remote_code=True, **kwargs
                    )
                else:
                    raise

        return model

    def load_model_cross_encoder(self, model_path: str, **kwargs):
        """Load a CrossEncoder model from HDFS.

        Downloads the model from HDFS to a temporary directory and loads it
        using the sentence-transformers library.

        Parameters
        ----------
        model_path : str
            HDFS path to the CrossEncoder model directory.
        **kwargs : dict
            Additional keyword arguments to pass to CrossEncoder constructor.

        Returns
        -------
        CrossEncoder
            Loaded CrossEncoder model instance.

        Raises
        ------
        ImportError
            If sentence-transformers library is not installed.
        ValueError
            If model requires trust_remote_code=True (automatically handled).

        Examples
        --------
        >>> client = HDFSClient()
        >>> model = client.load_model_cross_encoder(
        ...     "/dev/aiml/models/jina/jina-reranker-multilingual-base-v2"
        ... )
        >>> scores = model.predict([
        ...     ("Query 1", "Document 1"),
        ...     ("Query 2", "Document 2")
        ... ])
        >>>
        >>> # Load with custom settings
        >>> model = client.load_model_cross_encoder(
        ...     "/dev/aiml/models/jina/jina-reranker-multilingual-base-v2",
        ...     max_length=512
        ... )

        Notes
        -----
        The model is downloaded to a temporary directory which is automatically
        cleaned up after loading. If the model requires trust_remote_code=True,
        it will be automatically enabled with a warning.
        """
        try:
            from sentence_transformers import CrossEncoder
        except ImportError:
            raise ImportError(
                "`sentence-transformers` library is not installed, please install it"
            )

        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp_dir:
            local_model_path = f"{temp_dir}/model_dir"
            logger.debug("Downloading CrossEncoder model from HDFS: %s", model_path)
            self.download(model_path, local_model_path)
            logger.debug("Model downloaded to temporary path: %s", local_model_path)
            try:
                logger.info("Loading CrossEncoder from %s", local_model_path)
                model = CrossEncoder(local_model_path, **kwargs)
            except ValueError as exc:
                if "trust_remote_code=True" in str(exc):
                    logger.warning(
                        "Model requires trust_remote_code=True, enabling automatically."
                    )
                    model = CrossEncoder(
                        local_model_path, trust_remote_code=True, **kwargs
                    )
                else:
                    raise

        return model


####################################################################################################
FILE: src\jobs\evaluate_rag_job.py
####################################################################################################

"""
Offline RAG evaluation job.

Runs the full RAG pipeline (retrieval + LLM synthesis + guardrails) against
a golden Q&A dataset and scores every answer with the from-scratch,
heuristic :class:`~dlh_ai.genai.rag.RagEvaluator` (no ``ragas``, no
``datasets``, no LLM-as-judge). Prints a summary table and writes a CSV
report; exits with a non-zero status if any aggregate metric falls below
the thresholds configured in ``config.toml`` under ``[testing]``, usable
as a CI regression gate.

Run
---
::

    python src/jobs/evaluate_rag_job.py
"""

import logging
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import pandas as pd
from rich.console import Console
from rich.table import Table

from dlh_ai.config import get_settings
from dlh_ai.genai.prompt import PromptManager
from dlh_ai.genai.rag import (
    ChatRequest,
    EmbeddingService,
    GuardrailService,
    LlmService,
    RagEvaluator,
    RetrievalService,
    build_es_client,
    evaluate_dataset,
)

logger = logging.getLogger("dlh_ai")
console = Console()


def _load_golden_dataset(csv_path: str) -> list[dict]:
    path = Path(csv_path)
    if not path.is_file():
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"Golden Q&A dataset not found at '{csv_path}'. Configure "
            "'testing.golden_qa_dataset_path' in config.toml."
        )
    df = pd.read_csv(path)
    missing_columns = {"question", "ground_truth"} - set(df.columns)
    if missing_columns:
        raise ValueError(
            f"Golden Q&A dataset '{csv_path}' is missing required column(s): "
            f"{sorted(missing_columns)}"
        )
    return df[["question", "ground_truth"]].to_dict(orient="records")


def _build_retrieval_service() -> tuple[RetrievalService, EmbeddingService]:
    settings = get_settings()

    es_client = build_es_client(
        host=settings.rag.elasticsearch_host,
        api_key=settings.rag.elasticsearch_api_key,
        request_timeout=settings.rag.request_timeout,
        max_retries=settings.rag.max_retries,
        verify_certs=settings.rag.verify_certs,
        connections_per_node=settings.rag.connections_per_node,
    )

    embedding_service = EmbeddingService(
        hdfs_model_path=settings.rag.hdfs_model_path,
        expected_dimension=settings.rag.embedding_dimension,
        use_fp16=settings.rag.embedding_fp16,
        gpu_batch_size=settings.rag.embedding_gpu_batch_size,
        dynamic_batching=False,
    )

    llm_service = LlmService(
        model_name=settings.llm.model_name,
        base_url=settings.llm.base_url,
        api_key=settings.llm.api_key,
        verify_ssl=settings.llm.verify_ssl,
        temperature=settings.llm.temperature,
        max_tokens=settings.llm.max_tokens,
        request_timeout=settings.llm.request_timeout,
        connection_pool_size=settings.llm.connection_pool_size,
        max_retries=settings.llm.max_retries,
        use_max_completion_tokens=settings.llm.use_max_completion_tokens,
    )
    llm_service.load()

    guardrail_service = GuardrailService(
        pii_entities=settings.guardrails.pii_entities,
        enable_pii_redaction=settings.guardrails.enable_pii_redaction,
        enable_profanity_filter=settings.guardrails.enable_profanity_filter,
        enable_toxicity_filter=settings.guardrails.enable_toxicity_filter,
        toxicity_threshold=settings.guardrails.toxicity_threshold,
        grounding_threshold=settings.guardrails.grounding_threshold,
        detoxify_checkpoint_path=settings.guardrails.detoxify_checkpoint_path,
    )

    retrieval_service = RetrievalService(
        client=es_client,
        embedding_service=embedding_service,
        llm_service=llm_service,
        index_name=settings.rag.index_name,
        prompt_manager=PromptManager(),
        guardrail_service=guardrail_service,
    )
    return retrieval_service, embedding_service


def _print_summary(report) -> None:  # noqa: ANN001 (EvaluationReport, avoid import cycle in signature)
    table = Table(title="RAG Evaluation Summary (from-scratch heuristic metrics)")
    table.add_column("Metric")
    table.add_column("Mean", justify="right")
    table.add_column("Min", justify="right")

    table.add_row("Faithfulness", f"{report.faithfulness_mean:.4f}", f"{report.faithfulness_min:.4f}")
    table.add_row(
        "Context precision",
        f"{report.context_precision_mean:.4f}",
        f"{report.context_precision_min:.4f}",
    )
    table.add_row(
        "Context recall",
        f"{report.context_recall_mean:.4f}",
        f"{report.context_recall_min:.4f}",
    )
    table.add_row(
        "Answer relevance",
        f"{report.answer_relevance_mean:.4f}",
        f"{report.answer_relevance_min:.4f}",
    )
    console.print(table)


def main() -> int:
    settings = get_settings()

    logger.info("Loading golden Q&A dataset from '%s'", settings.testing.golden_qa_dataset_path)
    golden_records = _load_golden_dataset(settings.testing.golden_qa_dataset_path)

    logger.info("Initialising RAG pipeline for evaluation...")
    retrieval_service, embedding_service = _build_retrieval_service()
    evaluator = RagEvaluator(embedding_service=embedding_service)

    logger.info("Running %d golden queries through the RAG pipeline...", len(golden_records))
    records: list[dict] = []
    for row in golden_records:
        request = ChatRequest(query=row["question"])
        response = retrieval_service.answer_query(request)
        records.append(
            {
                "query": row["question"],
                "answer": response.answer,
                "contexts": [hit.answer for hit in response.sources],
                "ground_truth": row["ground_truth"],
            }
        )

    report = evaluate_dataset(records, evaluator)
    _print_summary(report)

    report_rows = [
        {
            "query": result.query,
            "answer": result.answer,
            "ground_truth": result.ground_truth,
            "faithfulness": result.faithfulness,
            "context_precision": result.context_precision,
            "context_recall": result.context_recall,
            "answer_relevance": result.answer_relevance,
        }
        for result in report.results
    ]
    report_path = Path("rag_evaluation_report.csv")
    pd.DataFrame(report_rows).to_csv(report_path, index=False)
    logger.info("Per-record report written to '%s'", report_path)

    thresholds = settings.testing
    failures: list[str] = []
    if report.faithfulness_mean < thresholds.faithfulness_threshold:
        failures.append(
            f"faithfulness_mean {report.faithfulness_mean:.4f} < "
            f"threshold {thresholds.faithfulness_threshold:.4f}"
        )
    if report.answer_relevance_mean < thresholds.answer_relevancy_threshold:
        failures.append(
            f"answer_relevance_mean {report.answer_relevance_mean:.4f} < "
            f"threshold {thresholds.answer_relevancy_threshold:.4f}"
        )
    if report.context_precision_mean < thresholds.context_precision_threshold:
        failures.append(
            f"context_precision_mean {report.context_precision_mean:.4f} < "
            f"threshold {thresholds.context_precision_threshold:.4f}"
        )
    if report.context_recall_mean < thresholds.context_recall_threshold:
        failures.append(
            f"context_recall_mean {report.context_recall_mean:.4f} < "
            f"threshold {thresholds.context_recall_threshold:.4f}"
        )

    if failures:
        for failure in failures:
            logger.error("RAG evaluation threshold breach: %s", failure)
        return 1

    logger.info("RAG evaluation passed all configured thresholds.")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

FILE: src\jobs\example_job.py
####################################################################################################


import logging

logger = logging.getLogger("dlh_ai")

logger.info("This is example job")
